									end
									str = nil
								end

								if str ~= nil then
									warn("[Treadmill] Lepas treadmill gak kekonfirmasi dalam " .. "1.5s, steal dilanjut apa adanya.")
									return false
								end
								task.wait(0.12)
								return true
							end

							warn("[Treadmill] Gagal minta lepas dari treadmill sebelum steal:", v57)
							return false
						end
					end
				end
			end

			if type(v53.__SenaV4DecomBoostCleanup) == "function" then
				pcall(v53.__SenaV4DecomBoostCleanup)
			end

			tbl3 = {}
			flag3 = false

			v53.__SenaV4DecomBoostCleanup = function()
				for _, v54 in ipairs(tbl3) do
					pcall(function()
						v54:Disconnect()
					end)
				end

				table.clear(tbl3)
				flag3 = false
			end

			do
				fn28 = function()
					local clientRenderedAssets = workspace:FindFirstChild("ClientRenderedAssets")

					if clientRenderedAssets then
						for _, child in ipairs(clientRenderedAssets:GetChildren()) do
							pcall(function()
								child.Parent = nil
							end)
						end

						return
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	do
		local fn29, fn30, fn31, fn32

		do
			do
				do
					fn29 = function(arg)
						return string.sub(arg.Name, 1, #tostring(localPlayer.UserId) + 1) == tostring(localPlayer.UserId) .. "_"
					end
				end

				fn30 = function()
					for _, child in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
						if child.Name == "PlacedEggRenders" then
							for _, child2 in ipairs(child:GetChildren()) do
								pcall(function()
									if not child2:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt") then
										if not fn29(child2) then
											child2.Parent = nil
										end
									end
								end)
							end
						end
					end
				end

				do
					fn31 = function()
						for _, v54 in ipairs({ "Transient", "__DEBRIS" }) do
							local v55 = workspace:FindFirstChild(v54)

							if v55 then
								for _, child in ipairs(v55:GetChildren()) do
									if child.Name ~= "PlayerTrap" then
										if not child.Name:lower():find("trap") then
											continue
										end
									end

									pcall(function()
										child:Destroy()
									end)
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			do
				fn24 = function(arg)
					if not arg:FindFirstAncestor("SenaVisualBack") then
						if not arg:FindFirstAncestor("SenaVisualRemote") then
							local tool = arg:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("Tool")
							return tool ~= nil and tool:GetAttribute("SenaVisual") ~= nil
						end
					end

					return true
				end
			end

			senaV4DecomBoostUndo = { effects = {}, parts = {}, decals = {}, snapshot = nil }

			do
				local tbl4 = {
					PlusOne = true,
					SpeedGainPopup = true,
					SellPayout = true,
					SyncedIncomeCash = true,
				}

				local tbl5 = {
					PlayerPlotSign = true,
					CanUpgrade = true,
					AlertGui = true,
					OfflineMoneyClaimBillboard = true,
				}

				fn32 = function(descendant)
					pcall(function()
						if fn24(descendant) then
							return
						end

						if tbl4[descendant.Name] then
							descendant:Destroy()
							return
						end

						if descendant:IsA("BillboardGui") and tbl5[descendant.Name] then
							if descendant.Enabled then
								senaV4DecomBoostUndo.effects[descendant] = true
							end

							descendant.Enabled = false
							return
						end

						if not descendant:IsA("ParticleEmitter") and not descendant:IsA("Trail") and not descendant:IsA("Beam") and not descendant:IsA("Sparkles") and not descendant:IsA("Fire") then
							if descendant:IsA("Smoke") or descendant:IsA("PostEffect") then
								if descendant.Enabled then
									senaV4DecomBoostUndo.effects[descendant] = true
								end

								descendant.Enabled = false
							end
						else
							if descendant.Enabled then
								senaV4DecomBoostUndo.effects[descendant] = true
							end

							descendant.Enabled = false
						end
					end)
				end
			end
		end

		local fn33

		do
			do
				local function fn34()
					if flag3 then
						return
					end
					flag3 = true
					table.insert(tbl3, game.DescendantAdded:Connect(fn32))

					table.insert(tbl3, workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
						if child.Name == "ClientRenderedAssets" then
							fn28()

							table.insert(tbl3, child.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child2)
								pcall(function()
									child2.Parent = nil
								end)
							end))
						elseif child.Name == "PlacedEggRenders" then
							fn30()

							table.insert(tbl3, child.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child2)
								pcall(function()
									if not child2:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt") then
										if not fn29(child2) then
											child2.Parent = nil
										end
									end
								end)
							end))
						elseif child.Name == "__DEBRIS" or child.Name == "Transient" then
							table.insert(tbl3, child.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child2)
								if child2.Name ~= "PlayerTrap" then
									if child2.Name:lower():find("trap") then
										pcall(function()
											child2:Destroy()
										end)
									end
								else
									pcall(function()
										child2:Destroy()
									end)

									return
								end
							end))
						end
					end))

					local clientRenderedAssets = workspace:FindFirstChild("ClientRenderedAssets")

					if clientRenderedAssets then
						table.insert(tbl3, clientRenderedAssets.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
							pcall(function()
								child.Parent = nil
							end)
						end))
					end

					for _, child in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
						if child.Name == "PlacedEggRenders" then
							table.insert(tbl3, child.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child2)
								pcall(function()
									if not child2:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt") then
										if not fn29(child2) then
											child2.Parent = nil
										end
									end
								end)
							end))
						end
					end
				end

				local flag4 = false

				fn25 = function()
					if flag4 then
						return
					end
					flag4 = true
					local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")

					if not senaV4DecomBoostUndo.snapshot then
						local tbl4 = {}

						pcall(function()
							tbl4.quality = settings().Rendering.QualityLevel
							tbl4.throttle = settings().Physics.PhysicsEnvironmentalThrottle
							tbl4.technology = Lighting.Technology
							tbl4.shadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows
							tbl4.fogEnd = Lighting.FogEnd
							tbl4.brightness = Lighting.Brightness
							tbl4.ambient = Lighting.Ambient
							tbl4.outdoorAmbient = Lighting.OutdoorAmbient
						end)

						local terrain = workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")

						if terrain then
							pcall(function()
								tbl4.terrain = {
									WaterWaveSize = terrain.WaterWaveSize,
									WaterWaveSpeed = terrain.WaterWaveSpeed,
									WaterReflectance = terrain.WaterReflectance,
									WaterTransparency = terrain.WaterTransparency,
									Decoration = terrain.Decoration,
								}
							end)

							pcall(function()
								local clouds = terrain:FindFirstChildOfClass("Clouds")

								if clouds then
									tbl4.cloudsEnabled = clouds.Enabled
								end
							end)
						end

						pcall(function()
							local preferences = ReplicatedStorage.Shared:FindFirstChild("Preferences")

							if preferences then
								local v54 = require(preferences)
								tbl4.prefs = { HideOtherPets = v54.IsOn("HideOtherPets"), DisableVideos = v54.IsOn("DisableVideos") }
							end
						end)

						if tbl4.quality == Enum.QualityLevel.Level01 then
							tbl4.quality = Enum.QualityLevel.Automatic
						end

						senaV4DecomBoostUndo.snapshot = v53.__SenaV4DecomSnapshot or tbl4
						v53.__SenaV4DecomSnapshot = senaV4DecomBoostUndo.snapshot
					end

					pcall(function()
						local level01 = Enum.QualityLevel.Level01
						settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = level01
						local always = Enum.EnviromentalPhysicsThrottle.Always
						settings().Physics.PhysicsEnvironmentalThrottle = always
						Lighting.Technology = Enum.Technology.Compatibility
						Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
						Lighting.FogEnd = 9e9
						Lighting.Brightness = 2
						Lighting.Ambient = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
						Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
					end)

					local v54 = os.clock()

					for _, v55 in ipairs({ workspace, Lighting }) do
						for _, descendant in ipairs(v55:GetDescendants()) do
							if not fn24(descendant) then
								if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
									pcall(function()
										if not senaV4DecomBoostUndo.parts[descendant] then
											local parts = senaV4DecomBoostUndo.parts
											local v56 = descendant
											local tbl4 = {}
											local material2 = descendant.Material
											local castShadow = descendant.CastShadow
											local renderFidelity2 = descendant:IsA("MeshPart") and descendant.RenderFidelity or nil
											tbl4[1] = material2
											tbl4[2] = castShadow
											tbl4[3] = renderFidelity2
											parts[v56] = tbl4
										end

										descendant.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
										descendant.CastShadow = false

										if descendant:IsA("MeshPart") then
											descendant.RenderFidelity = Enum.RenderFidelity.Performance
										end
									end)
								elseif descendant:IsA("Decal") then
									pcall(function()
										if descendant.Texture ~= "" then
											if not senaV4DecomBoostUndo.decals[descendant] then
												senaV4DecomBoostUndo.decals[descendant] = descendant.Texture
											end
										end

										descendant.Texture = ""
									end)
								elseif descendant:IsA("Texture") then
									pcall(function()
										if descendant.Texture ~= "" then
											if not senaV4DecomBoostUndo.decals[descendant] then
												senaV4DecomBoostUndo.decals[descendant] = descendant.Texture
											end
										end

										descendant.Texture = ""
									end)
								else
									fn32(descendant)
								end

								if os.clock() - v54 > 0.004 then
									task.wait()
									v54 = os.clock()
								end
							end
						end
					end

					local terrain = workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")

					if terrain then
						pcall(function()
							terrain.WaterWaveSize = 0
							terrain.WaterWaveSpeed = 0
							terrain.WaterReflectance = 0
							terrain.WaterTransparency = 1
							terrain.Decoration = false
							local clouds = terrain:FindFirstChildOfClass("Clouds")

							if clouds then
								clouds.Enabled = false
							end
						end)
					end

					pcall(function()
						local shared = ReplicatedStorage.Shared
						local preferences = shared:FindFirstChild("Preferences")

						if preferences then
							local v55 = require(preferences)
							v55.Set("HideOtherPets", true)
							v55.Set("DisableVideos", true)
						else
							local v55 = require(shared.SettingsCmds)
							v55.Write("HideOtherPets", true)
							v55.Write("DisableVideos", true)
						end
					end)

					fn28()
					fn30()
					fn31()
					fn34()
					flag4 = false
				end
			end

			senaV4DecomBoostUndo.revert = function()
				for _, v54 in ipairs(tbl3) do
					pcall(function()
						v54:Disconnect()
					end)
				end

				table.clear(tbl3)
				flag3 = false
				local snapshot = senaV4DecomBoostUndo.snapshot
				local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")

				if snapshot then
					pcall(function()
						settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = snapshot.quality or Enum.QualityLevel.Automatic
					end)

					pcall(function()
						local throttle = snapshot.throttle
						settings().Physics.PhysicsEnvironmentalThrottle = throttle
					end)

					pcall(function()
						Lighting.Technology = snapshot.technology
						Lighting.GlobalShadows = snapshot.shadows
						Lighting.FogEnd = snapshot.fogEnd
						Lighting.Brightness = snapshot.brightness
						Lighting.Ambient = snapshot.ambient
						Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = snapshot.outdoorAmbient
					end)

					local terrain = workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")

					if terrain then
						if snapshot.terrain then
							for k, v54 in pairs(snapshot.terrain) do
								pcall(function()
									terrain[k] = v54
								end)
							end

							if snapshot.cloudsEnabled ~= nil then
								pcall(function()
									local clouds = terrain:FindFirstChildOfClass("Clouds")

									if clouds then
										clouds.Enabled = snapshot.cloudsEnabled
									end
								end)
							end
						end
					end

					if snapshot.prefs then
						pcall(function()
							local v54 = require(ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Preferences)

							for k, pref in pairs(snapshot.prefs) do
								v54.Set(k, pref)
							end
						end)
					end
				end

				local n = 0

				for k, part in pairs(senaV4DecomBoostUndo.parts) do
					if k.Parent then
						pcall(function()
							k.Material = part[1]
							k.CastShadow = part[2]

							if part[3] ~= nil then
								if k:IsA("MeshPart") then
									k.RenderFidelity = part[3]
								end
							end
						end)
					end

					n += 1

					if n % 400 == 0 then
						task.wait()
					end
				end

				for k, decal in pairs(senaV4DecomBoostUndo.decals) do
					if k.Parent then
						pcall(function()
							k.Texture = decal
						end)
					end
				end

				for k in pairs(senaV4DecomBoostUndo.effects) do
					if k.Parent then
						pcall(function()
							k.Enabled = true
						end)
					end
				end

				senaV4DecomBoostUndo.parts = {}
				senaV4DecomBoostUndo.decals = {}
				senaV4DecomBoostUndo.effects = {}
				senaV4DecomBoostUndo.snapshot = nil
			end

			v53.__SenaV4DecomBoostUndo = senaV4DecomBoostUndo
			flag2 = false

			do
				fn33 = function(arg)
					if not arg or not arg:IsA("Model") then
						return
					end
					local match2 = arg.Name:match("^(%d+)_")
					local v54 = match2 and tonumber(match2)
					if not v54 or v54 == localPlayer.UserId then
						return
					end

					for _, descendant in ipairs(arg:GetDescendants()) do
						if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
							descendant.Transparency = 1
							descendant.CanCollide = false
						elseif descendant:IsA("Decal") or descendant:IsA("Texture") then
							descendant.Transparency = 1
						elseif descendant:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or descendant:IsA("Trail") or descendant:IsA("Beam") then
							descendant.Enabled = false
						elseif descendant:IsA("BillboardGui") or descendant:IsA("SurfaceGui") then
							descendant.Enabled = false
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		do
			fn26 = function()
				if senaV4Config._fpsEggsReady then
					return true
				end
				local flag4 = false

				local v54, v55 = pcall(function()
					local v54 = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Shared.Eggs.PlacedEggRenderer).GetRenderFolder()

					if v54 then
						local children = v54:GetChildren()

						for _, child in ipairs(children) do
							fn33(child)
						end

						v54.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
							task.defer(fn33, child)
						end)

						flag4 = true
						return
					end
				end)

				if v54 then
					return flag4
				end
				warn("[FPSBoost] Gagal hide egg pemain lain:", v55)
				return false
			end
		end
	end

	fn27 = function(arg)
		if arg then
			pcall(function()
				arg.Parent = nil
			end)

			return
		end
	end
end

local fn28, fn29, tbl3, v54, v55, v56, v57, fn30, fn31

do
	do
		local tbl4, v58, v59, v60, v61, fn32

		do
			do
				do
					do
						local fn33, fn34, fn35, fn36

						do
							do
								do
									fn33 = function()
										if senaV4Config._fpsPetsReady then
											return true
										end
										local isOn = nil

										local v62, v63 = pcall(function()
											local shared = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Shared
											local preferences = shared:FindFirstChild("Preferences")

											if preferences then
												local v62 = require(preferences)
												v62.Set("HideOtherPets", true)
												isOn = v62.IsOn
											else
												local v62 = require(shared.SettingsCmds)
												v62.Write("HideOtherPets", true)
												isOn = v62.Allows
											end
										end)

										local flag3 = false

										if v62 then
											if isOn then
												local hideOtherPets, v64 = pcall(isOn, "HideOtherPets")
												flag3 = hideOtherPets and v64 == true
											end
										end

										if v62 then
											if flag3 then
												local flag4 = false

												local v64, v65 = pcall(function()
													local clientRenderedAssets = workspace:FindFirstChild("ClientRenderedAssets") or workspace:WaitForChild("ClientRenderedAssets", 10)

													if clientRenderedAssets then
														local children = clientRenderedAssets:GetChildren()

														for _, child in ipairs(children) do
															fn27(child)
														end

														clientRenderedAssets.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
															task.defer(fn27, child)
														end)

														flag4 = true
														return
													end
												end)

												if v64 then
													return flag4
												end
												warn("[FPSBoost] Gagal hide pet (manual):", v65)
												return false
											end
										end

										warn("[FPSBoost] Gagal set HideOtherPets bawaan game:", v63 or "setting belum aktif")
										return false
									end
								end

								do
									fn34 = function()
										if type(v53.__SenaV4SetCapFps) == "function" then
											pcall(v53.__SenaV4SetCapFps, 240)
										end

										pcall(function()
											local level01 = Enum.QualityLevel.Level01
											settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = level01
										end)

										local function fn37()
											UserSettings():GetService("UserGameSettings").SavedQualityLevel = Enum.SavedQualitySetting.QualityLevel1
										end

										pcall(fn37)

										pcall(function()
											require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Client.LightingsController):SetModifier("FPSBoost", function(arg)
												local v62 = table.clone(arg)
												v62.Bloom = nil
												v62.ColorCorrection = nil
												v62.SunRays = nil
												v62.DepthOfField = nil
												v62.Blur = nil
												v62.Clouds = nil
												v62.Atmosphere = nil
												v62.ShadowSoftness = 1
												return v62
											end, 100, 0)
										end)

										pcall(function()
											local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
											Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
											Lighting.Technology = Enum.Technology.Compatibility
											Lighting.FogEnd = 9e9
											Lighting.Brightness = 2
										end)

										pcall(function()
											local terrain = workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")

											if terrain then
												terrain.WaterWaveSize = 0
												terrain.WaterWaveSpeed = 0
												terrain.WaterReflectance = 0
												terrain.WaterTransparency = 1
												terrain.Decoration = false
											end
										end)

										pcall(function()
											local debris = workspace:FindFirstChild("__DEBRIS")

											if debris then
												for _, child in ipairs(debris:GetChildren()) do
													if child.Name == "PlayerTrap" then
														pcall(function()
															child:Destroy()
														end)
													end
												end
											end
										end)
									end
								end
							end

							do
								local v62 = Color3.fromRGB(120, 120, 120)

								fn35 = function(arg)
									if arg and arg:IsA("BasePart") then
										arg.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
										arg.Color = v62
										arg.CastShadow = false
										return
									end
								end
							end

							do
								local function fn37(arg)
									if arg:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
										arg.Enabled = false
										arg.Rate = 0

										pcall(function()
											arg:Clear()
										end)
									elseif arg:IsA("Trail") then
										arg.Enabled = false
									elseif not arg:IsA("Beam") and not arg:IsA("Smoke") and not arg:IsA("Fire") and not arg:IsA("Sparkles") and not arg:IsA("PostEffect") and not arg:IsA("Light") and not arg:IsA("Highlight") then
										if arg:IsA("Clouds") then
											arg.Enabled = false
											return
										elseif arg:IsA("Atmosphere") then
											arg.Density = 0
											arg.Haze = 0
											arg.Glare = 0
										elseif arg:IsA("Explosion") then
											pcall(function()
												arg.Visible = false
											end)
										end
									else
										arg.Enabled = false
										return
									end
								end

								fn36 = function()
									if senaV4Config._fpsWorldReady then
										return true
									end
									local flag3 = false
									local tbl5 = {}

									local v62, v63 = pcall(function()
										local function fn38(arg)
											if fn24(arg) then
												return
											end
											fn35(arg)
											fn37(arg)
										end

										local tbl6 = { workspace, game:GetService("Lighting") }

										for _, v62 in ipairs(tbl6) do
											table.insert(tbl5, v62.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
												task.defer(fn38, descendant)
											end))
										end

										local tbl7 = {}

										for _, v62 in ipairs(tbl6) do
											for _, child in ipairs(v62:GetChildren()) do
												table.insert(tbl7, child)
											end
										end

										local n = 0

										while #tbl7 > 0 do
											local v62 = table.remove(tbl7)
											fn38(v62)

											for _, child in ipairs(v62:GetChildren()) do
												table.insert(tbl7, child)
											end

											n += 1

											if n >= 250 then
												if #tbl7 > 0 then
													n = 0
													RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
												end
											end
										end

										flag3 = true

									index._restoreClaimPresentationFilter = function(arg)
										local claimPresentationNode = arg.claimPresentationNode
										local claimPresentationOriginal = arg.claimPresentationOriginal

										if type(claimPresentationNode) == "table" then
											if type(claimPresentationOriginal) == "function" then
												local claimPresentationWrapper = arg.claimPresentationWrapper

												if rawget(claimPresentationNode, "_fn") == claimPresentationWrapper then
													claimPresentationNode._fn = claimPresentationOriginal
												end
											end
										end

										arg.claimPresentationNode = nil
										arg.claimPresentationOriginal = nil
										arg.claimPresentationWrapper = nil
										arg.claimPresentationSuppression = nil
									end

									index._setStatus = function(arg, status)
										if arg.stats.status == status then
											return
										end

										if type(status) == "string" then
											if string.find(status, "not currently trusted", 1, true) then
												arg._untrustedSeen = true
											end
										end

										arg.stats.status = status
										arg:_notify()
									end

									index._notify = function(arg)
										local v76 = arg:getSnapshot()

										for k in pairs(arg.listeners) do
											local v77, v78 = pcall(k, v76)

											if not v77 then
												warn("[StealAnEgg] UI listener failed:", v78)
											end
										end
									end

									index.onChanged = function(arg, arg2)
										arg.listeners[arg2] = true
										local v76, v77 = pcall(arg2, arg:getSnapshot())

										if not v76 then
											warn("[StealAnEgg] UI listener failed:", v77)
										end

										local function fn64()
											arg.listeners[arg2] = nil
										end

										return fn64
									end

									index.getSnapshot = function(arg)
										local tbl10 = {
											status = arg.stats.status,
											available = arg.stats.available,
											matching = arg.stats.matching,
											lastTarget = arg.stats.lastTarget,
											lastResult = arg.stats.lastResult,
										}

										local resetBlocked = arg.resetBlocked or v66.IsSealed()

										if not resetBlocked then
											local gateOpenAt = arg.gateOpenAt
											resetBlocked = os.clock() < gateOpenAt
										end

										tbl10.wallClosed = resetBlocked
										tbl10.enabled = arg.enabled
										tbl10.running = arg.running
										return tbl10
									end

									index._replaceSnapshot = function(arg, arg2)
										table.clear(arg.records)
										local records = arg2 and (arg2.Records or arg2) or {}

										for k, record in pairs(records) do
											if type(record) == "table" and type(record.Uid) == "string" then
												arg.records[record.Uid] = record
											elseif type(k) == "string" then
												if type(record) == "table" then
													arg.records[k] = record
												end
											end
										end

										arg:_refreshCounts()
										arg:_schedule()
									end

									index._indexWants = function(arg)
										local v76 = os.clock()
										local indexCache = arg.indexCache

										if indexCache then
											if v76 - (indexCache.at or 0) < 2 then
												return indexCache.set
											end
										end

										local tbl10 = {}

										pcall(function()
											local areasDirectory = arg.areasDirectory

											if not areasDirectory then
												areasDirectory = require(ReplicatedStorage2.Data.Areas).Directory
												arg.areasDirectory = areasDirectory
											end

											if areasDirectory then
												if v77 then
													local index2 = v77.Index or {}
													local tbl11 = {}
													local v78 = pairs
													local eggInventory = v77.EggInventory or {}

													for _, v79 in v78(eggInventory) do
														local assetCategory = type(v79) == "table" and (v79.AssetCategory or v79.Category) or nil

														if assetCategory then
															tbl11[tostring(assetCategory)] = true
														end
													end

													local uid = arg.carry and arg.carry.uid

													if uid then
														local v79 = arg.records[uid]

														if v79 then
															if v79.AssetCategory then
																tbl11[tostring(v79.AssetCategory)] = true
															end
														end
													end

													for _, v79 in pairs(areasDirectory) do
														if type(v79) == "table" then
															if type(v79.DropTable) == "table" then
																for _, v80 in ipairs(v79.DropTable) do
																	local v81 = v80[1]

																	if v81 then
																		local v82 = tostring(v81)

																		if not index2[v82] then
																			if not tbl11[v82] then
																				tbl10[v82] = true
																			end
																		end
																	end
																end
															end
														end
													end

													return
												end
											end
										end)

										arg.indexCache = { at = v76, set = tbl10 }
										return tbl10
									end

									index._isIndexObjective = function(arg, arg2)

											if type(arg2) ~= "table" then

											end

										end

									end

									index.setIndexObjective = function(arg, arg2)

											return false
										end

											local v76 = arg.catalog.resolve(arg2)
											if not v76 or v76.rarity ~= wispObjective.rarity then
												return false
											end
										end

									end

											return
										end

										arg.wispObjective = wispObjectiveKey and arg2 or nil
										arg:_reconcileRunningState()
									end

									index.indexMissingCount = function(arg)

											n += 1
										end

										return n
									end

									index._matchesNormalFilter = function(arg, arg2)
										local v76 = arg.catalog.resolve(arg2)
										local config = arg.config
										if config.rarityEnabled and (#config.rarities == 0 or not fn54(config.rarities, v76.rarity)) then
											return false
										end

										if config.nameEnabled and (#config.names == 0 or not fn54(config.names, v76.name) and not fn54(config.names, v76.category)) then
											return false
										end

										if config.areaEnabled and (#config.areas == 0 or not fn54(config.areas, arg2.AreaId)) then
											return false
										end

										if config.mutationEnabled and not fn63(arg2, config.mutations) then
											return false
										end

										if (config.minWeightKg or 0) > 0 then
											local v77 = arg:_resolveWeightKg(arg2)
											if not v77 or v77 < config.minWeightKg then
												return false
											end
										end

										if (config.minValuePerSec or 0) > 0 then
											local v77 = fn37(arg2)
											if not v77 or v77 < config.minValuePerSec then
												return false
											end
										end

										return true
									end
								end

								index._matches = function(arg, arg2)
									if arg2.State ~= v64.States.Slot and arg2.State ~= v64.States.Dropped then
										return false
									end

									if arg.riftWants[arg2.AssetCategory] then
										return true
									end

									if arg.indexObjectiveEnabled and arg:_isIndexObjective(arg2) then
										return true
									end

									if arg.wispObjective and arg:_isWispObjective(arg2) then
										return true
									end

									if arg.userStealEnabled and not arg.riftOnly and arg:_matchesNormalFilter(arg2) then
										return true
									end
									return false
								end

								index.setScrambleWants = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
									local riftWants = {}
									local v75 = ipairs
									local tbl8 = type(arg2) == "table" and arg2 or {}

									for _, v76 in v75(tbl8) do
										if type(v76) == "string" then
											if v76 ~= "" then
												riftWants[v76] = true
											end
										end
									end

									local riftOnly = arg3 == true
									local flag3 = riftOnly ~= arg.riftOnly

									for k in pairs(riftWants) do
										if not arg.riftWants[k] then
											flag3 = true
										end
									end

									for k in pairs(arg.riftWants) do
										if not riftWants[k] then
											flag3 = true
										end
									end

									if flag3 then
										arg.riftWants = riftWants
										arg.riftOnly = riftOnly
										arg:_reconcileRunningState()
										return
									end
								end

								index._refreshCounts = function(arg)
									local available = 0
									local matching = 0

									for _, record in pairs(arg.records) do
										if record.State == v64.States.Slot then
											available += 1

											if arg:_matches(record) then
												matching += 1
											end
										end
									end

									arg.stats.available = available
									arg.stats.matching = matching
									arg:_notify()
								end

								index.hasPriorityRareEgg = function(arg)
									local character = localPlayer2.Character
									character = character and localPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
									local v75 = nil
									local v76 = nil
									local v77 = nil
									local v78 = pairs
									local records = arg.records or {}

									for _, record in v78(records) do
										if record.State == v64.States.Slot or record.State == v64.States.Dropped then
											if record.BottomCFrame then
												if not arg:isSkippedEgg(record.Uid) then
													if arg:_matchesNormalFilter(record) then
														local v79 = arg.catalog.resolve(record)

														if tbl5[v79.rarity] then
															local magnitude = character and (record.BottomCFrame.Position - character.Position).Magnitude or 0
															local rarityRank = v79.rarityRank or 0

															if v75 and not (v76 < rarityRank) then
																if rarityRank ~= v76 then
																	continue
																end

																if not (magnitude < v77) then
																	continue
																end
															end

															v75 = record
															v76 = rarityRank
															v77 = magnitude
														end
													end
												end
											end
										end
									end

									return v75 ~= nil, v75
								end

								index.isSkippedEgg = function(arg, arg2)
									if arg2 ~= nil then
										if arg2 == workspace:GetAttribute("Event_CaptureTheEggUid") then
											if not arg.captureObjectiveEnabled then
												return true
											end
										end
									end

									local v75 = arg2 and arg.skippedEggs[arg2]

									if v75 then
										if os.clock() < v75 then
											return true
										end
										arg.skippedEggs[arg2] = nil
										return false
									end

									return false
								end

								index.setCaptureObjective = function(arg, arg2)
									local flag3 = arg2 == true
									if arg.captureObjectiveEnabled == flag3 then
										return
									end

									if flag3 then
										arg._modeBeforeCapture = { arg.config.stealMode, arg.config.flyAerial, arg.config.flyZigzag }
										arg:setStealMode("Instant", false, false)
										arg.captureObjectiveEnabled = true
									else
										arg.captureObjectiveEnabled = false
										local modeBeforeCapture = arg._modeBeforeCapture
										arg._modeBeforeCapture = nil

										if modeBeforeCapture then
											arg:setStealMode(modeBeforeCapture[1], modeBeforeCapture[2], modeBeforeCapture[3])
										end
									end

									arg:_reconcileRunningState()
								end

								index._captureHold = function(arg, captureHoldUid)
									if arg._captureHoldUid == captureHoldUid then
										return
									end
									arg._captureHoldUid = captureHoldUid

									local function fn53()
										local v75 = v62.ReadFieldEgg(captureHoldUid)
										return v75 ~= nil and v75.State == v64.States.Carried and v75.CarrierUserId == localPlayer2.UserId
									end

									local function fn54()
										return not arg.destroyed and arg.enabled and arg.captureObjectiveEnabled and fn53()
									end

									task.spawn(function()
										arg:_clearArrivalHold(captureHoldUid)
										arg:_hideTreadmillDecoy()

										pcall(function()
											arg:_stopHoverPad()
										end)

										arg.stats.lastResult = "Holding capture egg"
										arg:_setStatus("Holding capture egg")
										local character = localPlayer2.Character
										local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
										local cFrame = humanoidRootPart

										if cFrame then
											local rotation = humanoidRootPart.CFrame.Rotation
											cFrame = CFrame.new(humanoidRootPart.Position.X, 110, humanoidRootPart.Position.Z) * rotation
										end

										if cFrame then
											arg:_glideRoot(cFrame, function()
												return not fn54()
											end)
										end

										while fn54() do
											local character2 = localPlayer2.Character
											local humanoidRootPart2 = character2 and character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
											local humanoid = character2 and character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

											if humanoidRootPart2 then
												if humanoid then
													if cFrame then
														pcall(function()
															humanoidRootPart2.CFrame = cFrame
															humanoidRootPart2.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
															humanoidRootPart2.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
														end)

														arg:_syncHoverPad(cFrame.Position)
														local v75 = math.clamp(arg.config.speed or 600, 200, fn34())

														pcall(function()
															fn43(character2, v75 * 1.35)
															fn50(humanoid, humanoidRootPart2, v75, Vector3.zero)
														end)
													end
												end
											end

											task.wait(0.1)
										end

										pcall(function()
											arg:_stopHoverPad()
										end)

										if arg._captureHoldUid == captureHoldUid then
											arg._captureHoldUid = nil
										end

										if arg.destroyed or not arg.enabled then
											return
										end
										arg:_finish(arg.captureObjectiveEnabled and "Capture egg lost — re-grabbing" or "Capture stopped")
									end)
								end

								index._chooseTarget = function(arg)
									local humanoidRootPart = localPlayer2.Character and localPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

									if humanoidRootPart then
										if arg.captureObjectiveEnabled then
											local attribute = workspace:GetAttribute("Event_CaptureTheEggUid")
											local v75 = attribute and (v62.ReadFieldEgg(attribute) or arg.records[attribute])
											if type(v75) == "table" and v75.BottomCFrame and (v75.State == v64.States.Slot or v75.State == v64.States.Dropped) then
												return v75
											end
										end

										if arg.manualObjectiveEnabled then
											local v75 = arg:_takeManualTarget()
											if v75 then
												return v75
											end
										end

										if arg.userStealEnabled and not arg.riftOnly then
											local v75, v76 = arg:hasPriorityRareEgg()
											if v75 and v76 then
												return v76
											end
										end

										local v75 = nil
										local v76 = nil
										local v77 = nil
										local v78 = nil
										local v79 = nil
										local v80 = nil

										for _, record in pairs(arg.records) do
											if record.State == v64.States.Slot or record.State == v64.States.Dropped then
												if record.BottomCFrame then
													if not arg:isSkippedEgg(record.Uid) then
														local magnitude = (record.BottomCFrame.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude

														if arg.riftWants[record.AssetCategory] then
															if not v75 or magnitude < v76 then
																v75 = record
																v76 = magnitude
															end
														elseif arg.wispObjective and arg:_isWispObjective(record) then
															if not v79 or magnitude < v80 then
																v79 = record
																v80 = magnitude
															end
														elseif arg.indexObjectiveEnabled then
															if arg:_isIndexObjective(record) then
																if not v77 or magnitude < v78 then
																	v77 = record
																	v78 = magnitude
																end
															end
														end
													end
												end
											end
										end

										if v75 or v79 or v77 then
											return v75 or v79 or v77
										end

										if arg.userStealEnabled and not arg.riftOnly then
											local v81 = nil
											local v82 = nil
											local v83 = nil

											for _, record in pairs(arg.records) do
												if record.State == v64.States.Slot or record.State == v64.States.Dropped then
													if not arg:isSkippedEgg(record.Uid) then
														if arg:_matchesNormalFilter(record) then
															local magnitude = (record.BottomCFrame.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
															local v84 = arg.catalog.resolve(record)
															local n

															if arg.config.highestValueFirst then
																n = fn37(record) or -math.huge
															elseif arg.config.heaviestFirst then
																n = arg:_resolveWeightKg(record) or -math.huge
															else
																n = v84.rarityRank or 0
															end

															if v81 and not (v82 < n) then
																if n ~= v82 then
																	continue
																end

																if not (magnitude < v83) then
																	continue
																end
															end

															v81 = record
															v82 = n
															v83 = magnitude
														end
													end
												end
											end

											if v81 then
												return v81
											end
										end

										return nil
									end

									return nil
								end

								index._takeManualTarget = function(arg)
									local tbl8 = {}
									local manualOrder = {}

									for _, v75 in ipairs(arg.manualOrder) do
										local v76 = arg.records[v75]

										if v76 then
											if v76.State == v64.States.Slot or v76.State == v64.States.Dropped then
												manualOrder[#manualOrder + 1] = v75
												tbl8[v75] = true
											end
										end
									end

									arg.manualOrder = manualOrder

									for k in pairs(arg.manualQueue) do
										if not tbl8[k] then
											arg.manualQueue[k] = nil
										end
									end

									if #manualOrder == 0 then
										if arg.manualObjectiveEnabled then
											arg.manualObjectiveEnabled = false

											task.defer(function()
												if not arg.destroyed then
													arg:_reconcileRunningState()
												end
											end)
										end

										return nil
									end

									return arg.records[manualOrder[1]]
								end

								index.queueManual = function(arg, arg2)
									if type(arg2) ~= "table" then
										return 0
									end

									for _, v75 in ipairs(arg2) do
										local v76 = v75

										if v76 ~= nil then
											if not arg.manualQueue[v76] then
												arg.manualQueue[v76] = true
												arg.manualOrder[#arg.manualOrder + 1] = v76
											end
										end
									end

									local n = #arg.manualOrder

									if n > 0 then
										arg.manualObjectiveEnabled = true
									end

									arg:_reconcileRunningState()
									return n
								end

								do
									index.clearManual = function(arg)
										arg.manualQueue = {}
										arg.manualOrder = {}
										arg.manualObjectiveEnabled = false
										arg:_reconcileRunningState()
									end
								end

								end

								index.setTreadmillManager = function(arg, treadmillManager)
									arg.treadmillManager = treadmillManager
								end

								index.getStealMode = function(arg)
									return arg.config.stealMode == "Fly" and "Fly" or "Instant"
								end

								do
									index.setStealMode = function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
										local stealMode = arg2 == "Fly" and "Fly" or "Instant"
										local flyAerial = stealMode == "Fly" and arg3 == true
										local flyZigzag = stealMode == "Fly" and arg4 == true and not flyAerial

										if arg.config.stealMode ~= stealMode or arg.config.flyAerial ~= flyAerial or arg.config.flyZigzag ~= flyZigzag then
											arg.config.stealMode = stealMode
											arg.config.flyAerial = flyAerial
											arg.config.flyZigzag = flyZigzag

											if arg.enabled then
												arg:_installGuardHitDistanceBoost()
												arg:_finish("Mode changed")
											end
										end

										return stealMode
									end
								end

								do
									local resolveHitDistance = nil

									index._installGuardHitDistanceBoost = function(arg)
										if arg.guardHitBoostInstalled then
											return
										end

										pcall(function()
											local v75 = require(ReplicatedStorage2.Shared.Modules.GuardAreas.GuardChasePolicy)

											if v75 then
												if type(v75.ResolveHitDistance) == "function" then
													resolveHitDistance = resolveHitDistance or v75.ResolveHitDistance
													local v76 = arg

													v75.ResolveHitDistance = function(arg2)
														if v76.enabled and v76.instantPhase == "priming" then
															return 25
														end

														if v76.enabled or v76._recalling then
															return -math.huge
														end
														return resolveHitDistance and resolveHitDistance(arg2) or arg2 or 10
													end

													arg.guardHitBoostInstalled = true
												end
											end
										end)
									end

									index._removeGuardHitDistanceBoost = function(arg)
										if arg.guardHitBoostInstalled then
											pcall(function()
												local v75 = require(ReplicatedStorage2.Shared.Modules.GuardAreas.GuardChasePolicy)

												if v75 then
													if resolveHitDistance then
														v75.ResolveHitDistance = resolveHitDistance
														arg.guardHitBoostInstalled = false
													end
												end
											end)

											return
										end
									end
								end

								index._setInstantAnchored = function(arg, anchored)
									pcall(function()
										local humanoidRootPart = localPlayer2.Character and localPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

										if humanoidRootPart then
											if humanoidRootPart.Anchored ~= anchored then
												humanoidRootPart.Anchored = anchored
											end
										end
									end)
								end

								do
									index._kbCancelTimer = function(arg)
										if arg.hitTimer then
											pcall(task.cancel, arg.hitTimer)
											arg.hitTimer = nil
										end
									end
								end

								index._isPrimerEgg = function(arg, arg2)
									if arg2 == nil then
										return false
									end

									if arg2 == arg.primerUid then
										return true
									end
									local primerUids = arg.primerUids and arg.primerUids[arg2]

									if primerUids then
										if os.clock() < primerUids then
											return true
										end
									end

									if primerUids then
										arg.primerUids[arg2] = nil
									end

									return false
								end

								index._cleanupInstantSteal = function(arg)
									arg.instantPhase = nil
									arg.primerUid = nil
									arg.targetUid = nil
									arg._flyDeparting = false
									arg:_stopGuardStick()
									arg:_kbCancelTimer()
									arg:_setInstantAnchored(false)
									arg:_hideTreadmillDecoy()
								end

								index._choosePrimerEgg = function(arg, arg2)
									local v75 = fn()
									local areas = v75 and v75:FindFirstChild("Areas")
									local guardAreas = areas and areas:FindFirstChild("GuardAreas")
									local forest = guardAreas and guardAreas:FindFirstChild("Forest")
									local guard = forest and forest:FindFirstChild("Guard")
									local humanoidRootPart = guard and guard:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
									local position = humanoidRootPart and humanoidRootPart.Position
									local v76 = nil
									local v77 = nil

									for _, record in pairs(arg.records) do
										if record.State == v64.States.Slot then
											if record.AreaId == "Forest" then
												if record.Uid ~= arg2 then
													if not arg:_isCaptureEgg(record.Uid) then

								index._knockbackLanded = function()
									local character = localPlayer2.Character
									local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
									local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
									if not humanoidRootPart or not humanoid then
										return false
									end

									if humanoid.PlatformStand or humanoid.Sit then
										return true
									end
									local state = humanoid:GetState()
									if state ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll and state ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown and state ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding then
										return humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude > 18
									end
									return true
								end

								index._settleAfterKnockback = function(arg)
									local guardStrikeAnchor = arg.guardStrikeAnchor
									local n = os.clock() + 0.35

									while true do
										if not (os.clock() < n) or arg.destroyed then
											return
										else
											local character = localPlayer2.Character
											local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

											if humanoidRootPart then
												local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

												if humanoid then
													pcall(function()
														humanoid.PlatformStand = false
														humanoid.Sit = false
														humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
													end)
												end

												if guardStrikeAnchor then
													humanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(guardStrikeAnchor)
												end

												humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
												humanoidRootPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
												arg:_syncHoverPad(guardStrikeAnchor or humanoidRootPart.Position)
												RunService2.Heartbeat:Wait()
												continue
											end

											break
										end
									end
								end

																break
															end
															continue
														end

														break
													end

													break
												end

												break
											end

											break
										end

											local v77 = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

								end

								index._isInSafeArea = function(arg, arg2)

									return false
								end

								index._isInField = function(arg, arg2)
									local v75 = fn()
									local areas = v75 and v75:FindFirstChild("Areas")
									local wallStartCollision = areas and areas:FindFirstChild("WallStartCollision")
									if wallStartCollision and wallStartCollision:IsA("BasePart") then
										return arg2.Position.X >= wallStartCollision.Position.X + 3
									end
									return true
								end

								index._needsSafeZoneHop = function(arg, arg2)
									if arg:_isInField(arg2) or arg:_isInSafeArea(arg2) then
										return false, nil
									end
									return true, arg:_resolveSafeCFrame(arg2)
								end

								index._invalidateCarryWorker = function(arg)
									arg.carryWorker = nil
								end

								index._claimCarryWorker = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
									local generation = arg.carry.generation
									local carryWorker = arg.carryWorker

									if carryWorker then
										if carryWorker.generation == generation then
											return nil
										end
									end

									local carryWorker2 = { kind = arg2, uid = arg3, generation = generation, token = {} }
									arg.carryWorker = carryWorker2
									return carryWorker2
								end

								do
									index._isCarryWorkerCurrent = function(arg, arg2)
										return type(arg2) == "table" and arg.carryWorker == arg2 and arg.carry.generation == arg2.generation and not arg.destroyed and (arg.enabled or arg._externalDeliver)
									end
								end

								index._releaseCarryWorker = function(arg, arg2)
									if arg.carryWorker == arg2 then
										arg.carryWorker = nil
									end
								end

								index.ownsCarryRecovery = function(arg, arg2)
									if arg.destroyed or not arg.enabled then
										return false
									end

									if arg.carry.uid == arg2 then
										return true
									end
									return arg.running and arg.activeTarget ~= nil and (arg2 == nil or arg.activeTarget.Uid == arg2)
								end

								index.requestCarryRecovery = function(arg, arg2)
									if arg.destroyed or not arg.enabled or type(arg2) ~= "string" or arg2 == "" then
										return false
									end

									if arg:isSkippedEgg(arg2) then
										return true
									end

									if arg.tweenRecoveryUid == arg2 then
										arg:_scheduleMovementRetry()
										return true
									end

									if arg.carry.active and arg.carry.uid == arg2 then
										return true
									end
									local v75 = v62.ReadFieldEgg(arg2) or arg.records[arg2]
									if type(v75) ~= "table" or v75.State ~= v64.States.Dropped and v75.State ~= v64.States.Slot then
										return false
									end
									local carryWorker = arg.carryWorker

									if (not carryWorker or carryWorker.kind ~= "re-steal" or carryWorker.uid ~= arg2) and (not arg.activeTween or not arg.activeTarget or arg.activeTarget.Uid ~= arg2) then
										if not arg.carryAttemptToken or not arg.activeTarget or arg.activeTarget.Uid ~= arg2 then
											local carry = arg.carry
											carry.generation = carry.generation + 1
											arg:_invalidateCarryWorker()
											arg.carryAttemptToken = nil
											arg.running = true
											arg.activeTarget = v75

											task.defer(function()
												if not arg.destroyed then
													if arg.enabled then
														if not arg.carry.active then
															arg:_reStealDroppedEgg(arg2)
														end
													end
												end
											end)

											return true
										end
									end

									return true
								end

								index._hasConfirmedCarry = function(arg, arg2)
									if arg.carry.active then
										if arg2 ~= nil and arg.carry.uid ~= nil then
											return arg.carry.uid == arg2
										end
										return true
									end

									return false
								end

								index._adoptCarryFromRecord = function(arg, uid)
									if arg.carry.active and arg.carry.uid == uid then
										return
									end

									if arg.carryTimeouts then
										arg.carryTimeouts[uid] = nil
									end

									if arg.instantPhase == "priming" and uid == arg.primerUid then
										arg.carry.active = true
										arg.carry.uid = uid
										return
									end

									local carry = arg.carry
									carry.generation = carry.generation + 1
									arg:_invalidateCarryWorker()
									arg:_clearArrivalHold(uid)
									local activeTarget = arg.activeTarget
									arg.claimPresentationSuppression = { assetCategory = activeTarget and activeTarget.AssetCategory, expiresAt = os.clock() + 8 }
									arg.carry.active = true
									arg.carry.uid = uid
									arg.carryStartTime = os.clock()
									local humanoidRootPart = localPlayer2.Character and localPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
									arg.carryStartPos = activeTarget and activeTarget.BottomCFrame and activeTarget.BottomCFrame.Position or humanoidRootPart and humanoidRootPart.Position

									if arg.tweenRecoveryUid == uid then
										arg.tweenRecoveryUid = nil
									end

									arg.stats.lastResult = "Carry confirmed"
									arg:_notify()
								end

								index._recordShowsCarried = function(arg, arg2)
									if type(arg2) ~= "string" or arg2 == "" then
										return false
									end
									local v75 = v62.ReadFieldEgg(arg2) or arg.records[arg2]
									return type(v75) == "table" and v75.State == v64.States.Carried and v75.CarrierUserId == localPlayer2.UserId
								end

								index._recordShowsForeignCarry = function(arg, arg2)
									if type(arg2) ~= "string" or arg2 == "" then
										return false
									end
									local v75 = v62.ReadFieldEgg(arg2) or arg.records[arg2]
									return type(v75) == "table" and v75.State == v64.States.Carried and v75.CarrierUserId ~= nil and v75.CarrierUserId ~= localPlayer2.UserId
								end

								index._resolveMovementSpeed = function(arg, arg2)
									if arg2.Parent and not (arg2.Health <= 0) then
										return math.clamp(arg.config.speed, 200, fn34())
									end
									return nil
								end

									return true
								end

								index._feetOffset = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
									if arg3.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 then
										return arg2.Size.Y * 0.5 + math.max(arg3.HipHeight, 0.5)
									end
									return arg2.Size.Y * 0.5 + 2
								end

								index.setAntiTrap = function(arg, arg2)
									arg.config.antiTrap = arg2 == true

									if arg.config.antiTrap then
										arg:_purgeTraps()
									end
								end

								index._isForeignTrap = function(arg, arg2)
									if arg2.Name == "PlayerTrap" and arg2:IsA("BasePart") then
										local attribute = arg2:GetAttribute("Owner")
										return type(attribute) ~= "string" or attribute ~= localPlayer2.Name
									end
									return arg2:IsA("Model") and arg2.Name == "Trap" and arg2:FindFirstChild("Hitbox") ~= nil
								end

								index._purgeTrap = function(arg, arg2)
									if arg.config.antiTrap and arg2 then
										if arg2.Parent then
											if arg:_isForeignTrap(arg2) then
												pcall(arg2.Destroy, arg2)
											end

											return
										end
									end
								end

								index._purgeTraps = function(arg)
									for _, v75 in ipairs({ "Transient", "__DEBRIS" }) do
										local v76 = workspace:FindFirstChild(v75)

										if v76 then
											for _, descendant in ipairs(v76:GetDescendants()) do
												arg:_purgeTrap(descendant)
											end
										end
									end
								end

								do
									index._syncHoverPad = function(arg, arg2)
										local hoverPad = arg.hoverPad

										if hoverPad and hoverPad.Parent then
											local hoverPadOwnerId = arg.hoverPadOwnerId

											if hoverPad:GetAttribute("SenaV4RuntimeOwner") == hoverPadOwnerId then
												local character = localPlayer2.Character
												local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
												local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
												if not humanoidRootPart or not humanoid then
													return false
												end
												local position = typeof(arg2) == "Vector3" and arg2 or humanoidRootPart.Position
												local y = position.Y

												if arg.hoverPadHeightOverride ~= nil then
													if typeof(arg2) ~= "Vector3" then
														y = arg.hoverPadHeightOverride
													end
												end

												local n = 8

												if arg.hoverPadHeightOverride ~= nil then
													n = math.max(8, humanoid.WalkSpeed * 2 / 15)
												end

												if math.abs(hoverPad.Size.X - n) > 0.5 then
													hoverPad.Size = Vector3.new(n, 1, n)
												end

												local z = position.Z
												hoverPad.CFrame = CFrame.new(position.X, y - arg:_feetOffset(humanoidRootPart, humanoid) - 0.5 - 0.05, z)
												return true
											end
										end

										return false
									end
								end

								do
									index._setArrivalHold = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
										if typeof(arg2) ~= "CFrame" then
											return
										end
										local character = localPlayer2.Character
										local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
										if character and humanoidRootPart then
											arg.arrivalHold = { Character = character, RootPart = humanoidRootPart, CFrame = arg2, Uid = arg3 }
											return
										end
									end
								end

								index._clearArrivalHold = function(arg, arg2)
									local arrivalHold = arg.arrivalHold

									if not arrivalHold or arg2 == nil or arrivalHold.Uid == nil or arrivalHold.Uid == arg2 then
										arg.arrivalHold = nil
									end
								end

								index._enforceArrivalHold = function(arg)
									local arrivalHold = arg.arrivalHold
									if type(arrivalHold) ~= "table" or typeof(arrivalHold.CFrame) ~= "CFrame" then
										return false
									end

									if localPlayer2.Character == arrivalHold.Character and arrivalHold.RootPart and arrivalHold.RootPart.Parent then
										arg:_startHoverPad()

										pcall(function()
											arrivalHold.RootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
											arrivalHold.RootPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
											arg:_syncHoverPad(arrivalHold.CFrame.Position)
											arrivalHold.RootPart.CFrame = arrivalHold.CFrame
										end)

										arg:_syncHoverPad(arrivalHold.CFrame.Position)
										return true
									end

									arg.arrivalHold = nil
									return false
								end

								index._purgeOrphanHoverPads = function(arg)
									for _, child in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
										if child.Name == "__SenaV4HoverPad" then
											if child ~= arg.hoverPad then
												pcall(child.Destroy, child)
											end
										end
									end
								end

								index._startHoverPad = function(arg)
									local hoverPad = arg.hoverPad
									local Part

									if hoverPad and hoverPad.Parent then
										local hoverPadOwnerId = arg.hoverPadOwnerId

										if hoverPad:GetAttribute("SenaV4RuntimeOwner") ~= hoverPadOwnerId then
											arg:_purgeOrphanHoverPads()

											if hoverPad then
												if hoverPad.Parent then
													pcall(hoverPad.Destroy, hoverPad)
												end
											end

											Part = Instance.new("Part")
											Part.Name = "__SenaV4HoverPad"
											Part:SetAttribute("SenaV4RuntimeOwner", arg.hoverPadOwnerId)
											Part.Anchored = true
											Part.CanCollide = true
											Part.CanQuery = true
											Part.CanTouch = false
											Part.CastShadow = false
											Part.Transparency = 1
											Part.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
											Part.Size = Vector3.new(8, 1, 8)
											arg.hoverPad = Part
											Part.Parent = workspace
										end
									else
										arg:_purgeOrphanHoverPads()

										if hoverPad then
											if hoverPad.Parent then
												pcall(hoverPad.Destroy, hoverPad)
											end
										end

										Part = Instance.new("Part")
										Part.Name = "__SenaV4HoverPad"
										Part:SetAttribute("SenaV4RuntimeOwner", arg.hoverPadOwnerId)
										Part.Anchored = true
										Part.CanCollide = true
										Part.CanQuery = true
										Part.CanTouch = false
										Part.CastShadow = false
										Part.Transparency = 1
										Part.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
										Part.Size = Vector3.new(8, 1, 8)
										arg.hoverPad = Part
										Part.Parent = workspace
									end

									arg:_syncHoverPad()

									if not arg.hoverPadConnection then
										arg.hoverPadConnection = RunService2.PreSimulation:Connect(function()
											if not arg:_syncHoverPad() then
												arg:_startHoverPad()
											end
										end)
									end

									if not arg.hoverPadPostConnection then
										arg.hoverPadPostConnection = RunService2.PostSimulation:Connect(function()
											if arg.activeTween or arg.arrivalHold then
												arg:_syncHoverPad()
											end
										end)
									end
								end

								do
									index._stopHoverPad = function(arg)
										arg.hoverPadHeightOverride = nil

										if arg.hoverPadConnection then
											pcall(arg.hoverPadConnection.Disconnect, arg.hoverPadConnection)
											arg.hoverPadConnection = nil
										end

										if arg.hoverPadPostConnection then
											pcall(arg.hoverPadPostConnection.Disconnect, arg.hoverPadPostConnection)
											arg.hoverPadPostConnection = nil
										end

										if arg.hoverPad then
											pcall(arg.hoverPad.Destroy, arg.hoverPad)
											arg.hoverPad = nil
										end

										arg:_purgeOrphanHoverPads()
									end
								end

								do
									index._startMovementProtection = function(arg)
										local character = localPlayer2.Character
										local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
										local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
										if not character or not humanoidRootPart or not humanoid then
											return false
										end
										local v75 = math.clamp(arg.config.speed or 600, 200, fn34())
										arg.protectionCharacter = character
										arg.protectionHumanoid = humanoid
										arg.protectionRoot = humanoidRootPart
										fn43(character, v75 * 1.35)
										fn49(humanoid, humanoidRootPart, v75)

										if not arg.integritySpoofConnection then
											arg.integritySpoofConnection = RunService2.PreSimulation:Connect(function()
												local character2 = localPlayer2.Character
												local humanoidRootPart2 = character2 and character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
												local humanoid2 = character2 and character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
												if not character2 or not humanoidRootPart2 or not humanoid2 then
													return
												end
												local v76 = math.clamp(arg.config.speed or 600, 200, fn34())
												arg.protectionCharacter = character2
												arg.protectionHumanoid = humanoid2
												arg.protectionRoot = humanoidRootPart2
												fn43(character2, v76 * 1.35)

												if not fn50(humanoid2, humanoidRootPart2, v76) then
													fn49(humanoid2, humanoidRootPart2, v76)
												end

												arg:_enforceArrivalHold()
											end)
										end

										if not arg.arrivalHoldConnection then
											arg.arrivalHoldConnection = RunService2.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
												arg:_enforceArrivalHold()
											end)
										end

										return true
									end
								end

								index._startFlyGrounding = function(arg)
									arg:_stopFlyGrounding()
									local v75 = fn46()

									arg._flyGroundConn = RunService2.PreSimulation:Connect(function()
										if arg.destroyed then
											return
										end
										local state = v75 and v75.State
										if type(state) ~= "table" or rawget(state, "CorrectionContext") ~= nil then
											return
										end
										rawset(state, "IsSupportedNow", true)
										rawset(state, "UnsupportedStartedAt", nil)

										if rawget(state, "MovementMode") ~= "Correcting" then
											rawset(state, "MovementMode", "Grounded")
										end

										local evidence = rawget(state, "Evidence")

										if type(evidence) == "table" then
											evidence.Flight = 0
											evidence.Teleport = 0
											evidence.Speed = 0
										end
									end)
								end

								index._stopFlyGrounding = function(arg)
									if arg._flyGroundConn then
										pcall(arg._flyGroundConn.Disconnect, arg._flyGroundConn)
										arg._flyGroundConn = nil
									end
								end

								index._stopMovementProtection = function(arg)
									arg:_stopFlyGrounding()

									if arg.integritySpoofConnection then
										pcall(arg.integritySpoofConnection.Disconnect, arg.integritySpoofConnection)
										arg.integritySpoofConnection = nil
									end

									if arg.arrivalHoldConnection then
										pcall(arg.arrivalHoldConnection.Disconnect, arg.arrivalHoldConnection)
										arg.arrivalHoldConnection = nil
									end

									arg:_clearArrivalHold()
									arg:_stopHoverPad()
									fn44()
									fn51(arg.protectionHumanoid)
									arg.protectionCharacter = nil
									arg.protectionHumanoid = nil
									arg.protectionRoot = nil
								end

								index.travelAndWait = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
									local tbl8 = arg3 or {}
									if arg.destroyed then
										return false
									end

									if arg.travelBusy then
										if os.clock() < (arg.travelBusyUntil or 0) then
											return false
										end
									end

									if arg.activeTween then
										if not arg.running then
											if not arg.activeTween.sharedTravel or tbl8.preempt == true then
												pcall(arg.activeTween.Cancel, arg.activeTween)
												local n = os.clock() + 0.5

												while arg.activeTween do
													if os.clock() < n then
														task.wait(0.05)
														continue
													end
													break
												end
											end
										end
									end

									if arg.activeTween then
										return false
									end
									local character = localPlayer2.Character
									local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

									if humanoidRootPart then
										arg.travelBusy = true
										arg.travelBusyUntil = os.clock() + 45

										local v75, v76 = pcall(function()
											local flag3 = nil
											local v75 = os.clock()
											local v76 = v75
											local magnitude = (arg2.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
											local n = v75 + magnitude / math.max(tonumber(tbl8.speed) or arg.config.speed or 600, 200) + 6
											arg.travelBusyUntil = n + 2

											arg:_tweenRoot(arg2, nil, tbl8.speed, {
												silent = true,
												sharedTravel = true,
												abort = function()
													return arg.destroyed or tbl8.abort ~= nil and tbl8.abort()
												end,
												onDone = function(arg4)
													flag3 = arg4 == true
												end,
											})

											while flag3 == nil do
												local v77 = os.clock()
												local humanoidRootPart2 = localPlayer2.Character and localPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
												local magnitude2 = humanoidRootPart2 and (arg2.Position - humanoidRootPart2.Position).Magnitude or math.huge

												if magnitude2 < magnitude - 0.5 then
													magnitude = magnitude2
													v76 = v77
												end

												if arg.destroyed or tbl8.abort ~= nil and tbl8.abort() or v77 - v76 >= 1.5 or n <= v77 then
													if arg.activeTween then
														if arg.activeTween.Cancel then
															pcall(arg.activeTween.Cancel)
														end
													end

													flag3 = false
													break
												end

												task.wait(0.05)
											end

											return flag3
										end)

										arg.travelBusy = false
										if v75 then
											return v76 == true
										end

										if arg.activeTween then
											if arg.activeTween.sharedTravel then
												if arg.activeTween.Cancel then
													pcall(arg.activeTween.Cancel)
												end
											end
										end

										return false
									end

									return false
								end

								do
									index._tweenRoot = function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4, arg5)
										arg:_clearArrivalHold()
										local character = localPlayer2.Character
										local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
										local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
										local tbl8 = arg5 or {}

										if humanoidRootPart and humanoid then
											local v75 = arg4 or arg:_resolveMovementSpeed(humanoid)

											if v75 then
												local v76 = math.clamp(v75, 200, fn34())
												arg:_startMovementProtection()
												pcall(fn38, character)
												local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera

												if currentCamera then
													if currentCamera.CameraSubject ~= humanoid then
														pcall(function()
															currentCamera.CameraSubject = humanoid
														end)
													end
												end

												local autoRotate = humanoid.AutoRotate
												humanoid.AutoRotate = false
												local flag3 = tbl8.flightProfile == true and arg:_resolveRouteCruiseY(humanoidRootPart, arg2) or nil

												local activeTween = {
													sharedTravel = tbl8.sharedTravel == true,
													routeCruiseY = flag3,
													travelPhase = flag3 and "takeoff" or nil,
													zigzag = arg.config.stealMode == "Zig-Zag",
													zigT = 0,
													zigLast = 0,
												}

												arg.activeTween = activeTween
												arg:_startHoverPad()
												local flag4 = false
												local connection = nil
												local v77 = nil
												local v78 = nil
												local n = 0

												local function fn55(arg6)
													if flag4 then
														return
													end
													flag4 = true
													v77 = nil

													if humanoid.Parent ~= nil then
														humanoid.AutoRotate = autoRotate
													end

													if arg6 then
														if tbl8.holdAfterArrival then
															arg:_setArrivalHold(arg2, tbl8.holdUid)
														end
													end

													if tbl8.onDone then
														pcall(tbl8.onDone, arg6 == true)
													end

													if connection then
														connection:Disconnect()
														connection = nil
													end

													if arg.activeTween == activeTween then
														arg.activeTween = nil
													end

													if not arg.arrivalHold then
														arg:_stopHoverPad()
													end

													if not arg.enabled then
														arg:_stopMovementProtection()
													end

													pcall(function()
														if localPlayer2.Character == character then
															if humanoidRootPart.Parent then
																humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
															end
														end
													end)

													if arg6 then
														if localPlayer2.Character == character then
															if humanoid.Parent ~= nil then
																if arg3 then
																	arg3()
																end
															end
														end
													end
												end

												activeTween.Cancel = function()
													fn55(false)
												end

												connection = RunService2.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
													if tbl8.abort and tbl8.abort() then
														fn55(false)
														return
													end

													if arg.destroyed or arg.activeTween ~= activeTween then
														fn55(false)
														return
													end

								index._requestCarry = function(arg, arg2, arg3)

																				end

										return not arg.destroyed and arg.enabled and v53.__SenaV4AdminBoost == true and arg._adminBusyUid == uid
									end

									local v75, v76 = pcall(function()
										arg.instantPhase = nil
										arg.primerUid = nil
										arg.targetUid = nil
										arg.activeTarget = activeTarget
										arg.stats.lastResult = "Admin Abuse"
										arg:_setStatus("Heading to target egg")

										local n2 = 0

										while fn56() and not fn55() do
											if n <= os.clock() then

											end

												if (humanoidRootPart.Position - v76.Position).Magnitude > 4 then
													arg:_teleportRoot(humanoidRootPart, humanoid, v76)
												end
											end
