												n2 = os.clock() + 0.1
												arg:_setStatus("Securing egg")

												task.spawn(function()
													pcall(function()
														eggWorld.AskFieldEggCarry:InvokeServer({ Uid = uid })
													end)
												end)
											end

											RunService2.Heartbeat:Wait()
										end

										if fn56() then
											arg.carry.active = true
											arg.carry.uid = uid
											arg:_adminDeliver(uid)
											return
										end
									end)

									if arg._adminBusyUid == uid then
										arg._adminBusyUid = nil
									end

									if not v75 then
										warn("[Sena Steal] admin steal error: " .. tostring(v76))

										if not arg.destroyed then
											if arg.enabled then
												arg:_finish("Steal error — retrying")
											end
										end
									end
								end

								do
									index._adminDeliver = function(arg, adminDeliverUid)
										if arg._adminDeliverUid == adminDeliverUid then
											return
										end
										arg._adminDeliverUid = adminDeliverUid
										arg._adminBusyUid = adminDeliverUid

										local function fn55()
											return v62.ReadFieldEgg(adminDeliverUid)
										end

										local function fn56()
											local v75 = fn55()
											return v75 ~= nil and v75.State == v64.States.Carried and v75.CarrierUserId == localPlayer2.UserId
										end

										local function fn57()
											local v75 = fn55()
											return v75 == nil or v75.State == v64.States.Claimed
										end

										arg:_clearArrivalHold(adminDeliverUid)
										arg:_setStatus("Bringing egg home")
										local n = os.clock() + 4

										while not arg.destroyed do
											if arg.enabled then
												if fn57() then
													break
												end

												if fn56() then
													if os.clock() < n then
														local character = localPlayer2.Character
														local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
														local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
														local v75 = humanoidRootPart and arg:_resolveSafeCFrame(humanoidRootPart)

														if v75 then
															if humanoid then
																if (humanoidRootPart.Position - v75.Position).Magnitude > 4 then
																	arg:_teleportRoot(humanoidRootPart, humanoid, v75)
																	arg:_setStatus("Waiting for deposit")
																end
															end
														end

														RunService2.Heartbeat:Wait()
														continue
													end

													break
												end

												break
											end

											break
										end

										arg._adminDeliverUid = nil

										if arg._adminBusyUid == adminDeliverUid then
											arg._adminBusyUid = nil
										end

										if arg.destroyed or not arg.enabled then
											return
										end

										if fn57() then
											arg:_reportStolen()
											arg:_finish("Safe return complete")
										else
											arg:_finish(fn56() and "Bank not confirmed — retrying" or "Egg lost — retrying")
										end
									end
								end

								do
									index._returnToSafe = function(arg)
										if arg:_isPrimerEgg(arg.carry.uid) then
											local uid = arg.carry.uid
											arg.stats.lastResult = "Steal not banked — retrying"
											arg.carry.active = false
											arg.carry.uid = nil
											local carry = arg.carry
											carry.generation = carry.generation + 1
											arg:_invalidateCarryWorker()
											arg.skippedEggs[uid] = os.clock() + 30
											pcall(v62.DropFieldEgg, "PlayerRequest")

											if arg.instantPhase == "priming" then
												arg:_startPrimerTargetHunt()
											else
												task.delay(0.5, function()
													if not arg.destroyed then
														if arg.enabled then
															if not arg.running then
																arg:_schedule()
															end
														end
													end
												end)
											end

											arg:_notify()
											return
										end

										if arg.captureObjectiveEnabled and arg:_isCaptureEgg(arg.carry.uid) then
											arg:_captureHold(arg.carry.uid)
											return
										end

										if v53.__SenaV4AdminBoost == true then
											local uid = arg.carry.uid or arg.activeTarget and arg.activeTarget.Uid

											if uid then
												if arg._adminBusyUid ~= uid then
													task.spawn(arg._adminDeliver, arg, uid)
												end
											end

											return
										end

										if arg.config.stealMode ~= "Instant" and arg.config.stealMode ~= "Fly" then
											local uid = arg.carry.uid
											local v75 = arg:_claimCarryWorker("return", uid)

											if v75 then
												arg:_clearArrivalHold(uid)

												if arg:_wallOpen() then
													local character = localPlayer2.Character
													local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

													if humanoidRootPart then
														if not arg:_hasConfirmedCarry(uid) or arg:_recordShowsForeignCarry(uid) then
															arg:_releaseCarryWorker(v75)
															arg:_finish("Carry not confirmed — retrying")
															return
														end

														if arg:_isInSafeArea(humanoidRootPart) then
															arg:_finalizeReturn(v75)
															return
														end

														if senaV4Config.HelpFriend then
															arg.handoffLane = (arg.handoffLane or 0) % #tbl6 + 1
															local rotation = humanoidRootPart.CFrame.Rotation
															local n = CFrame.new(612, v73.Y, tbl6[arg.handoffLane]) * rotation
															arg:_setStatus("Taking the egg to the friend drop")

															arg:_tweenRoot(n, function()
																if arg:_isCarryWorkerCurrent(v75) then
																	task.spawn(arg._handOffEgg, arg, v75, uid, n)
																end
															end, nil, { flightProfile = true })

															return
														end

														local v76 = arg:_resolveSafeCFrame(humanoidRootPart)

														if v76 then
															arg:_setStatus("Returning to safe area")

															arg:_tweenRoot(v76, function()
																if arg:_isCarryWorkerCurrent(v75) then
																	arg:_finalizeReturn(v75)
																end
															end, nil, { flightProfile = true })

															return
														end

														arg:_releaseCarryWorker(v75)
														arg:_finish("Safe destination unavailable")
														return
													end

													arg:_releaseCarryWorker(v75)
													arg:_finish("Character unavailable")
													return
												end

												arg:_releaseCarryWorker(v75)
												arg:_finish("Reset interrupted carry")
												return
											end

											return
										end

										local uid = arg.carry.uid or arg.activeTarget and arg.activeTarget.Uid
										if uid then
											arg:_instantReturn(uid)
											return
										end
										arg:_finish("Carry lost — retrying")
									end
								end

								do
									index._handOffEgg = function(arg, arg2, arg3, cFrame)
										local function fn55()
											local humanoidRootPart = localPlayer2.Character and localPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

											if humanoidRootPart then
												humanoidRootPart.CFrame = cFrame
												humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
												humanoidRootPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
												return true
											end

											return false
										end

										local function fn56()
											local v75 = v62.ReadFieldEgg(arg3)
											return v75 ~= nil and v75.State == v64.States.Carried and v75.CarrierUserId == localPlayer2.UserId
										end

										arg:_setStatus("Dropping the egg for a friend")
										local n = 0.1

										pcall(function()
											n = localPlayer2:GetNetworkPing() or 0.1
										end)

										local n2 = os.clock() + math.max(0.3, 0.2 + 2 * n)

										while os.clock() < n2 do
											if fn55() then
												RunService2.Heartbeat:Wait()
												continue
											end
											break
										end

										arg.skippedEggs[arg3] = os.clock() + 1800
										arg:_reportStolen()
										local flag3 = false

										for i = 1, 4 do
											fn55()

											if fn56() then
												pcall(v62.DropFieldEgg, "PlayerRequest")
												local n3 = os.clock() + 0.6

												while true do
													RunService2.Heartbeat:Wait()
													flag3 = not fn56()

													if flag3 then
														break
													elseif n3 <= os.clock() then
														break
													end
												end

												if flag3 then
													break
												end
												continue
											end

											flag3 = true
											break
										end

										if flag3 then
											local n3 = os.clock() + 1

											while arg.carry.active do
												if os.clock() < n3 then
													RunService2.Heartbeat:Wait()
													continue
												end
												break
											end

											arg.carry.active = false
											arg.carry.uid = nil
											arg.stats.lastResult = "Dropped for a friend"
											local humanoidRootPart = localPlayer2.Character and localPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
											local v75 = humanoidRootPart and arg:_resolveSafeCFrame(humanoidRootPart)

											if v75 then
												arg:_setStatus("Egg dropped for a friend, heading back")

												arg:_tweenRoot(v75, nil, nil, {
													flightProfile = true,
													onDone = function()
														arg:_finish("Dropped for a friend")
													end,
												})

												return
											end

											arg:_finish("Dropped for a friend")
											return
										end

										arg.skippedEggs[arg3] = nil

										if arg:_isCarryWorkerCurrent(arg2) then
											arg:_releaseCarryWorker(arg2)
											arg:_finish("Friend drop failed — retrying")
										end
									end
								end

								do
									index._reportStolen = function(arg)
										local activeTarget = arg.activeTarget
										if type(activeTarget) ~= "table" or not activeTarget.Uid then
											return
										end

										if arg.reportedUid == activeTarget.Uid then
											return
										end
										arg.reportedUid = activeTarget.Uid

										task.delay(1.5, function()
											if arg.destroyed then
												return
											end
											local v75 = nil

											pcall(function()
												v75 = v62.ReadFieldEgg(activeTarget.Uid)
											end)

											if type(v75) == "table" and (v75.State == v64.States.Slot or v75.State == v64.States.Dropped or v75.State == v64.States.Carried and v75.CarrierUserId ~= localPlayer2.UserId) then
												arg.reportedUid = nil
												arg.stats.deliveryFailed = (arg.stats.deliveryFailed or 0) + 1
												arg.stats.lastResult = "Delivery failed — retrying"
												arg:_notify()

												if arg.enabled then
													if not arg.running then
														arg:_schedule()
													end
												end

												return
											end

											arg.stats.delivered = (arg.stats.delivered or 0) + 1
											local senaV4Webhook = v53.__SenaV4Webhook

											if senaV4Webhook then
												local v76, v77 = pcall(arg.catalog.resolve, activeTarget)
												pcall(senaV4Webhook.stolen, activeTarget, v76 and v77 or nil, nil)
												return
											end
										end)
									end
								end

								do
									index._startWatchdog = function(arg)
										if arg.watchdogRunning then
											return
										end
										arg.watchdogRunning = true

										task.spawn(function()
											local v75 = nil
											local v76 = os.clock()
											local v77 = nil
											local v78 = nil
											local position = nil
											local v79 = nil

											while fn2() do
												if arg.destroyed then
													break
												end

												if arg.userStealEnabled then
													task.wait(2)
													local humanoidRootPart = localPlayer2.Character and localPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
													local v80 = tostring(arg.stats.status)

													if arg.enabled and arg.running and not arg.carry.active and arg.carryAttemptToken == nil and arg._instantReturnToken == nil and not arg._captureHoldUid and humanoidRootPart and v80 == v78 and position and (humanoidRootPart.Position - position).Magnitude < 3 then
														if os.clock() - v79 > 12 then
															v78 = nil
															position = nil
															v79 = nil
															v75 = nil
															arg.stats.watchdogResets = (arg.stats.watchdogResets or 0) + 1

															pcall(function()
																arg:_invalidateCarryWorker()
															end)

															arg:_finish("Stuck - retrying")
															arg:_releaseOperation()
														end
													else
														position = humanoidRootPart and humanoidRootPart.Position
														v78 = v80
														v79 = os.clock()
													end

													if arg.enabled and not arg._captureHoldUid then
														if arg.running then
															v77 = nil
															local str = tostring(arg.activeTarget and arg.activeTarget.Uid) .. "|" .. tostring(arg.carry.active)

															if str ~= v75 then
																v75 = str
																v76 = os.clock()
															elseif os.clock() - v76 > 60 then
																v75 = nil
																arg.stats.watchdogResets = (arg.stats.watchdogResets or 0) + 1
																local uid = arg.activeTarget and arg.activeTarget.Uid

																if uid then
																	if not arg.carry.active then
																		arg.skippedEggs[uid] = os.clock() + 45

																		if arg.tweenRecoveryUid == uid then
																			arg.tweenRecoveryUid = nil
																		end
																	end
																end

																pcall(function()
																	arg:_invalidateCarryWorker()
																end)

																arg:_finish("Stuck — retrying")
															end
														else
															v75 = nil
															local v81, v82 = pcall(arg._chooseTarget, arg)

															if arg:_wallOpen() and v81 and v82 ~= nil then
																v77 = v77 or os.clock()

																if os.clock() - v77 > 6 then
																	v77 = nil
																	arg.stats.watchdogResets = (arg.stats.watchdogResets or 0) + 1
																	arg:_releaseOperation()
																	arg:_schedule()
																end
															else
																v77 = nil
															end
														end
													else
														v75 = nil
														v77 = nil
													end

													continue
												end

												break
											end

											arg.watchdogRunning = false
										end)
									end
								end

								index._finalizeReturn = function(arg, arg2)
									if arg:_isCarryWorkerCurrent(arg2) then
										if arg.carry.active then
											arg:_setStatus("Validating safe arrival")
											local v75 = os.clock()
											local n = os.clock() + 6

											while arg:_isCarryWorkerCurrent(arg2) and arg.carry.active and os.clock() < n do
												local humanoidRootPart = localPlayer2.Character and localPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

												if humanoidRootPart then
													if arg:_isInSafeArea(humanoidRootPart) then
														if os.clock() - v75 >= 0.35 then
															arg:_setStatus("Waiting for deposit")
														end

														task.wait(0.08)
														continue
													end

													arg:_setStatus("Rollback detected — returning again")
													arg:_releaseCarryWorker(arg2)

													task.defer(function()
														if not arg.destroyed then
															if arg.enabled then
																if arg.carry.active then
																	arg:_returnToSafe()
																end
															end
														end
													end)

													return
												end

												arg:_releaseCarryWorker(arg2)
												arg:_finish("Character unavailable")
												return
											end

											if arg:_isCarryWorkerCurrent(arg2) then
												if arg.carry.active then
													arg:_setStatus("Deposit pending — validating again")

													task.defer(function()
														if arg:_isCarryWorkerCurrent(arg2) then
															if arg.carry.active then
																arg:_finalizeReturn(arg2)
															end
														end
													end)

													return
												end

												arg:_releaseCarryWorker(arg2)
												arg:_reportStolen()
												arg:_finish("Safe return complete")
												return
											end

											return
										end

										arg:_releaseCarryWorker(arg2)
										arg:_reportStolen()
										arg:_finish("Safe return complete")
										return
									end
								end

								index._finish = function(arg, arg2)
									arg:_noteLowSpeedOutcome(arg2)
									arg:_hideTreadmillDecoy()
									arg._forestDropInProgress = nil
									arg._instantReturnToken = nil
									arg.boostedUid = nil
									arg:_invalidateCarryWorker()
									arg.planGeneration = arg.planGeneration + 1
									arg:_cancelTween(arg2, arg.enabled)
									arg:_setStatus(arg.enabled and "Searching" or "Idle")

									if arg.enabled then
										task.delay(0.05, function()
											if not arg.destroyed and arg.enabled then
												arg:_schedule()
												return
											end
											arg:_releaseOperation()
										end)
									end
								end

								index._scheduleMovementRetry = function(arg)
									if not arg.movementRetryPending and not arg.destroyed and arg.enabled then
										arg.movementRetryPending = true

										task.delay(0.2, function()
											arg.movementRetryPending = false

											if not arg.destroyed then
												if arg.enabled then
													arg:_schedule()
												end
											end
										end)

										return
									end
								end

								index._schedule = function(arg)
									if not arg.running then
										arg.safeStarting = false
									end

									if arg.destroyed or not arg.enabled or arg.running then
										return
									end
									local character = localPlayer2.Character
									local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
									local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

									if not humanoidRootPart or not humanoid then
										arg:_releaseOperation()
										arg:_setStatus("Character unavailable")
										return
									end

									arg:_startMovementProtection()

									if arg.carry.active and arg.carry.uid ~= nil then
										if arg.instantPhase == "priming" and arg.carry.uid == arg.primerUid then
											return
										end

										if arg:_isPrimerEgg(arg.carry.uid) then
											arg:_setStatus("Dropping primer egg")
											arg.skippedEggs[arg.carry.uid] = os.clock() + 30
											pcall(v62.DropFieldEgg, "PlayerRequest")

											task.delay(0.5, function()
												if not arg.destroyed then
													if arg.enabled then
														arg:_schedule()
													end
												end
											end)

											return
										end

										local v75 = arg.records[arg.carry.uid] or v62.ReadFieldEgg(arg.carry.uid)
										local captureObjectiveEnabled = type(v75) == "table" and (arg.riftWants[v75.AssetCategory] or arg.captureObjectiveEnabled and arg:_isCaptureEgg(v75.Uid) or arg.indexObjectiveEnabled and arg:_isIndexObjective(v75) or arg.wispObjective and arg:_isWispObjective(v75) or arg.userStealEnabled and not arg.riftOnly and arg:_matchesNormalFilter(v75))
										if type(v75) ~= "table" or captureObjectiveEnabled then
											arg:_returnToSafe()
											return
										end
										arg:_setStatus("Dropping unwanted egg")
										arg.skippedEggs[arg.carry.uid] = os.clock() + 30
										pcall(v62.DropFieldEgg, "PlayerRequest")

										task.delay(0.5, function()
											if not arg.destroyed then
												if arg.enabled then
													arg:_schedule()
												end
											end
										end)

										return
									end

									if arg:_wallOpen() then
										local senaV4PlayerSteal = v53.__SenaV4PlayerSteal
										local autoSteal = tbl4.blocker("auto_steal")
										local autoSteal2 = not autoSteal and tbl4.holder("auto_steal") or nil

										if not autoSteal and not autoSteal2 and (not senaV4PlayerSteal or not senaV4PlayerSteal.running) then
											local tweenRecoveryUid = arg.tweenRecoveryUid
											local v75 = tweenRecoveryUid and (v62.ReadFieldEgg(tweenRecoveryUid) or arg.records[tweenRecoveryUid]) or nil

											if tweenRecoveryUid then
												if type(v75) == "table" then
													if v75.State ~= v64.States.Slot then
														if v75.State ~= v64.States.Dropped then
															arg.tweenRecoveryUid = nil
															tweenRecoveryUid = nil
															v75 = nil
														end
													end
												else
													arg.tweenRecoveryUid = nil
													tweenRecoveryUid = nil
													v75 = nil
												end
											end

											v75 = v75 or arg:_chooseTarget()

											if v75 then
												if arg.treadmillManager then
													if arg.treadmillManager:isEngaged() then
														arg.treadmillManager:_cancelIdle(true)
													end
												end

												local planGeneration = arg.planGeneration
												local v76, v77 = arg:_acquireOperation()

												if v76 then
													arg.safeStarting = true
													arg.running = true
													arg.activeTarget = v75
													local v78 = arg.catalog.resolve(v75)
													arg.stats.lastTarget = string.format("%s · %s · %s", v78.rarity, v78.name, v75.AreaId)

													if tweenRecoveryUid then
														arg.safeStarting = false
														arg.stats.lastResult = "Recovering with tween"
														local v79 = arg:_resolveEggDestination(v75, humanoidRootPart, humanoid)

														if v79 then
															arg:_setStatus("Returning to egg")

															arg:_tweenRoot(v79, function()
																if arg.tweenRecoveryUid == v75.Uid then

								index._leftTreadmill = function(arg, arg2)
									local treadmillManager = arg.treadmillManager
									if not treadmillManager or treadmillManager.destroyed then
										return true
									end

											end

											arg.treadmillLeaveAt = nil
											return true
										end
										return false
									end

									index._reconcileRunningState = function(arg)

															end

															continue
														end

														break
													end

														return
													end
													arg._externalDeliver = nil

												return
											end

									index._neutralizeScramblePortal = function(arg, arg2)
										if typeof(arg2) ~= "Instance" or arg2.Name ~= "ScrambleArenaPortal" then
											return
										end
										arg.scramblePortalOriginals = arg.scramblePortalOriginals or {}

													if type(attribute) == "string" then
														arg.scramblePortalOriginals[descendant] = { CanCollide = attribute:sub(1, 1) == "1", CanTouch = attribute:sub(2, 2) == "1" }
													else
														arg.scramblePortalOriginals[descendant] = { CanCollide = descendant.CanCollide, CanTouch = descendant.CanTouch }
														pcall(descendant.SetAttribute, descendant, "SenaV4PortalOrig", (descendant.CanCollide and "1" or "0") .. (descendant.CanTouch and "1" or "0"))
													end
												end

													descendant.CanCollide = false
													descendant.CanTouch = false
												end)
											end
										end
									end
								end

									end

									if not arg.scramblePortalConn then

											if arg.destroyed or not arg.userStealEnabled or arg._scramblePortalHold or senaV4Config.ScrambleBossAuto == true then
												return
											end

											if child.Name ~= "ScrambleArenaPortal" then
												return

															if not arg._scramblePortalHold then

																	if child.Parent then
																		arg:_neutralizeScramblePortal(child)
																	end
																end
															end
														end
													end
												end)
											end
										end)
									end
								end

											end

									end

									local v75 = nil

									local function fn55(arg)
										if type(v75) ~= "table" then
											local v76, v77 = pcall(require, ReplicatedStorage2.Packages.FormatNumber.Simple)

											if v76 then
												if type(v77.FormatCompact) == "function" then
													v75 = v77
												end
											end
										end

										if v75 then
											local v76, v77 = pcall(v75.FormatCompact, arg, ".#")
											if v76 and type(v77) == "string" then
												return v77
											end
										end

										local v76 = math.abs(arg)
										if v76 >= 1e12 then
											return string.format("%.2fT", arg / 1e12)
										end

										if v76 >= 1e9 then
											return string.format("%.2fB", arg / 1e9)
										end

										if v76 >= 1000000 then
											return string.format("%.1fM", arg / 1000000)
										end

										if v76 >= 1000 then
											return string.format("%.1fK", arg / 1000)
										end
										return string.format("%d", math.floor(arg + 0.5))
									end

									index.settleNeed = function(arg, arg2, arg3)

									index.recommendLowSpeedLevel = function(arg, arg2)
										local v76 = arg2 or fn52()
										local tbl11 = {}

										if arg.config.areaEnabled and #arg.config.areas > 0 then
											tbl11 = arg.config.areas
										else
											for k in pairs(fn56()) do
												tbl11[#tbl11 + 1] = k
											end
										end

										local n = 10

											local v79 = arg:settleNeed(v78, v76)

											local n4 = 1

											for i = 10, 1, -1 do
												if v79 <= fn53(v78, i) then
													n3 = i
													break
												end
											end

											for i = 10, 1, -1 do
												if v79 * 1.25 <= fn53(v78, i) then
													n4 = i
													break
												end
											end

											if n3 < n2 then
												n2 = n3
												v77 = v78
											end

											n = math.min(n, n4)
										end

										local lowSpeedFailCap = arg.lowSpeedFailCap

										if lowSpeedFailCap then
											n2 = math.min(n2, lowSpeedFailCap)
											n = math.min(n, lowSpeedFailCap)
										end

										return math.min(n, n2), n2, v76, v77
									end

									index._noteLowSpeedOutcome = function(arg, arg2)
										local flag3 = arg._untrustedSeen == true
										arg._untrustedSeen = nil
										if arg.config.stealMode ~= "Instant" then
											return
										end

										if arg.boostedUid ~= nil or arg._boostHopUid ~= nil then
											return
										end
										local flag4 = tbl9[tostring(arg2)] == true or flag3
										if not flag4 and arg2 ~= "Safe return complete" then
											return
										end
										local v76 = math.clamp(tonumber(senaV4Config.StealHoldSeconds) or 5, 1, 10)
										arg.lowSpeedOutcomes = arg.lowSpeedOutcomes or {}
										local tbl11 = arg.lowSpeedOutcomes[v76] or {}
										arg.lowSpeedOutcomes[v76] = tbl11
										tbl11[#tbl11 + 1] = { at = os.clock(), failed = flag4 }

										while #tbl11 > 8 do
											table.remove(tbl11, 1)
										end

										local n = 0
										local n2 = 0

										for _, v77 in ipairs(tbl11) do
											local at = v77.at

											if os.clock() - at <= 600 then
												n2 += 1

												if v77.failed then
													n += 1
												end
											end

										end

										end

										local index = {}
										index.__index = index

										index.new = function(coordinator, travel)
											local v72 = setmetatable({}, index)
											v72.coordinator = coordinator
											v72.travel = travel
											v72.destroyed = false
											v72.listeners = {}
											v72.resources = {}
											v72.config = { disableDuringAutomation = true, idleFarm = false, autoUpgrade = false }
											v72.stats = { mode = "Idle", boost = "Waiting" }
											v72.busy = coordinator and coordinator:getSnapshot().busy == true or false
											v72.idleGeneration = 0
											v72.lastEquipAt = 0
											v72.activeTreadmill = nil
											v72.travelingToTreadmill = false
											v72.treadmillEquipPending = false
											v72.unequipGuardRunning = false
											v72.backgroundYieldRunning = false
											v72.lastBackgroundYieldAt = 0
											v72.carryPulseRunning = false
											v72.treadmillStateRevision = 0
											v72:_listenActiveTreadmill()

											task.spawn(function()
												pcall(function()
													v72:_syncActiveTreadmillSnapshot()
												end)
											end)

											v72:_listenCoordinator()
											v72:_listenSpeedGain()
											return v72
										end

										index._snapshot = function(arg)
											return {
												mode = arg.stats.mode,
												boost = arg.stats.boost,
												disableDuringAutomation = arg.config.disableDuringAutomation,
												idleFarm = arg.config.idleFarm,
											}
										end

										index._emit = function(arg)
											local v72 = arg:_snapshot()

											for _, listener in ipairs(arg.listeners) do
												pcall(listener, v72)
											end
										end

										index._setStatus = function(arg, mode, boost)
											if mode ~= nil then
												arg.stats.mode = mode
											end

											if boost ~= nil then
												arg.stats.boost = boost
											end

											arg:_emit()
										end

										index.subscribe = function(arg, arg2)
											table.insert(arg.listeners, arg2)
											pcall(arg2, arg:_snapshot())

											local function fn38()
												local v72 = table.find(arg.listeners, arg2)

												if v72 then
													table.remove(arg.listeners, v72)
												end
											end

											return fn38
										end

										index._requestUnequip = function()
											local v72, v73, v74 = pcall(function()
												return v67.Treadmill.AskDoff:InvokeServer()
											end)

											if v72 then
												if v73 == false then
													local flag3 = false
													local v75 = tostring
													local str = v74 or "Server rejected"
													return flag3, v75(str)
												end

												return true
											end

											return false, tostring(v73)
										end

										index._shouldBlockTreadmill = function(arg)
											if arg.destroyed or arg.carryPulseRunning then
												return false
											end

											if arg.busy and arg.config.disableDuringAutomation then
												return true
											end
											local senaV4EggRuntime = v53.__SenaV4EggRuntime
											if senaV4EggRuntime and senaV4EggRuntime._treadmillResetting then
												return false
											end
											local v72, v73 = pcall(tbl4.holder, "treadmill")
											return v72 and v73 ~= nil
										end

										index._startUnequipGuard = function(arg)
											if not arg.unequipGuardRunning and arg:_shouldBlockTreadmill() and arg.activeTreadmill ~= nil then
												arg.unequipGuardRunning = true

												task.spawn(function()
													local n = 0.15

													while arg:_shouldBlockTreadmill() do
														if arg.activeTreadmill ~= nil then
															local v72, v73 = arg:_requestUnequip()
															arg:_setStatus("Automation active", v72 and "Blocked" or "Release retry: " .. tostring(v73))
															task.wait(n)
															n = math.min(n * 2, 0.75)
															continue
														end

														break
													end

													arg.unequipGuardRunning = false

													if arg:_shouldBlockTreadmill() then
														if arg.activeTreadmill ~= nil then
															arg:_startUnequipGuard()
														end
													end
												end)

												return
											end
										end

										index._cancelIdle = function(arg, arg2)
											arg.idleGeneration = arg.idleGeneration + 1

											if arg2 then
												arg:_requestUnequip()
											end
										end

										index.setDisableDuringAutomation = function(arg)
											arg.config.disableDuringAutomation = true

											if arg.busy and arg.config.disableDuringAutomation then
												arg:_cancelIdle(true)
												arg:_startUnequipGuard()
												arg:_setStatus("Automation active", "Blocked")
											elseif not arg.busy then
												if arg.config.idleFarm then
													arg:_startIdle()
												end
											end
										end

										index.setIdleFarm = function(arg, arg2)
											arg.config.idleFarm = arg2 == true

											if arg.config.idleFarm then
												if not arg.busy then
													arg:_startIdle()
												end

												if arg.idleWatchdog then
													return
												end

												arg.idleWatchdog = task.spawn(function()
													while not arg.destroyed do
														if arg.config.idleFarm then
															task.wait(2)

															if arg.idleRunning == nil then
																if not arg.busy then
																	if arg.config.idleFarm then
																		if not arg.destroyed then
																			arg:_startIdle()
																		end
																	end
																end
															end

															continue
														end

														break
													end

													arg.idleWatchdog = nil
												end)

												return
											end

											arg:_cancelIdle(true)
											arg:_setStatus(arg.busy and "Automation active" or "Idle", "Disabled")
										end

										index._readUpgradeState = function()
											local v72, v73, v74, v75 = pcall(function()
												local v72 = (v70.Peek or v70.Get)()
												if v72 then
													local n = tonumber(v72.TreadmillUpgradeLevel) or 0
													return n, v72.Money, v71.GetByUpgradeLevel(n + 1)
												end
												return nil
											end)

											if v72 and v73 ~= nil then
												return v73, v74, v75
											end
											return nil
										end

										index._raiseTier = function(arg)
											local v72, v73, v74 = arg:_readUpgradeState()

											if v74 then
												if (tonumber(v73) or 0) < (tonumber(v74.Price) or 0) then
													return false, "Not enough money"
												end

												local v75, v76, v77 = pcall(function()
													return v67.Treadmill.AskTierRaise:InvokeServer(v74._id)
												end)

												if v75 then
													if v76 ~= true then
														local flag3 = false
														local v78 = tostring
														local str = v77 or "Server rejected"
														return flag3, v78(str)
													end

													local flag3 = true
													local displayName = v74.DisplayName or v74._id
													return flag3, displayName
												end

												return false, tostring(v76)
											end

											return false, "Max tier"
										end

										index.setAutoUpgrade = function(arg, arg2)
											arg.config.autoUpgrade = arg2 == true

											if arg.config.autoUpgrade and not arg.autoUpgradeRunning then
												arg.autoUpgradeRunning = true

												task.spawn(function()
													while not arg.destroyed do
														if arg.config.autoUpgrade then
															local v72, v73 = arg:_raiseTier()

															if v72 then
																arg:_setStatus(arg.busy and "Automation active" or "Idle", "Upgraded to " .. tostring(v73))
																task.wait(1)
															elseif v73 == "Max tier" then
																arg:_setStatus(arg.busy and "Automation active" or "Idle", "Treadmill maxed")
																task.wait(30)
															else
																task.wait(5)
															end

															continue
														end

														break
													end

													arg.autoUpgradeRunning = false
												end)

												return
											end
										end

										index._resolveStandCFrame = function()
											local v72 = v68.ResolveLocalSlot()
											local v73 = v68.ResolveFolder()
											local v74 = v73 and v72 and v73:FindFirstChild(tostring(v72))
											local treadmillBottom = v74 and v74:FindFirstChild("TreadmillBottom")
											if not treadmillBottom or not treadmillBottom:IsA("BasePart") then
												return nil
											end
											local character = localPlayer2.Character
											local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
											local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
											if not humanoidRootPart or not humanoid then
												return nil
											end
											local n

											if humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 then
												n = humanoidRootPart.Size.Y * 0.5 + math.max(humanoid.HipHeight, 0.5)
											else
												n = humanoidRootPart.Size.Y * 0.5 + 2
											end

											return treadmillBottom.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, treadmillBottom.Size.Y * 0.5 + n + 0.05, 0)
										end

										index.isEngaged = function(arg)
											return arg.activeTreadmill ~= nil or arg.travelingToTreadmill or arg.treadmillEquipPending or arg.carryPulseRunning
										end

										index._isTreadmillHudActive = function()
											local v72, v73 = pcall(function()
												local Game = v69.Frame("Game")
												local Treadmill = v69.Frame("Treadmill")
												return Game.Visible == false and Treadmill.Visible == true
											end)

											local flag3 = v72 and v73 == true
											return flag3
										end

										index._isGameHudRestored = function()
											local v72, v73 = pcall(function()
												local Game = v69.Frame("Game")
												local Treadmill = v69.Frame("Treadmill")
												return Game.Visible == true and Treadmill.Visible == false
											end)

											local flag3 = v72 and v73 == true
											return flag3
										end

										index.pulseForCarry = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
											if arg.destroyed or arg.carryPulseRunning or type(arg2) ~= "function" then
												return false, "Preparation unavailable"
											end
											arg:_cancelIdle(false)
											arg.carryPulseRunning = true

											local function fn38()
												return arg.destroyed or arg3 ~= nil and arg3()
											end

											local function fn39(arg4)
												local n = os.clock() + arg4
												local n2 = 0
												local v72 = nil
												local treadmillStateRevision = arg.treadmillStateRevision

												while true do
													if arg.destroyed or not (os.clock() < n) then
														return false
													else
														local v73 = os.clock()

														if n2 <= v73 then
															arg:_requestUnequip()
															n2 = v73 + 0.15
														end

														local v74 = arg:_syncActiveTreadmillSnapshot()
														local v75 = arg:_isGameHudRestored()

														if arg.treadmillStateRevision ~= treadmillStateRevision then
															treadmillStateRevision = arg.treadmillStateRevision
															v72 = nil
														end

														if not v74 and arg.activeTreadmill == nil and v75 then
															v72 = v72 or v73
															if v73 - v72 >= 0.35 then
																break
															end
														else
															v72 = nil
														end

														task.wait(0.03)
													end
												end

												return true
											end

											local function fn40(arg4, arg5)
												if not arg4 then
													fn39(1.85)
												end

												arg.treadmillEquipPending = false
												arg.carryPulseRunning = false

												if arg4 then
													arg:_setStatus("Automation active", "Preparation complete")
												elseif not arg.destroyed then
													arg:_setStatus("Automation active", tostring(arg5 or "Preparation failed"))
												end

												if arg:_shouldBlockTreadmill() then
													if arg.activeTreadmill ~= nil then
														arg:_startUnequipGuard()
													end
												end

												return arg4, arg5
											end

											arg:_syncActiveTreadmillSnapshot()
											local flag3 = false
											local str = nil

											for i = 1, 3 do
												if fn38() then
													return fn40(false, "Cancelled")
												end
												local v72 = arg:_resolveStandCFrame()

												if v72 then
													arg:_setStatus("Preparing carry", i == 1 and "Positioning" or "Repositioning")
													local v73, v74 = pcall(arg2, v72)

													if v73 and v74 == true then
														task.wait(0.35)
														arg.treadmillEquipPending = true
														arg:_setStatus("Preparing carry", "Activating")
														local n = os.clock() + 2.5
														local n2 = 0
														local n3 = 0

														while not fn38() do
															if os.clock() < n then
																local v75 = os.clock()

																if n3 <= v75 then
																	n3 = v75 + 0.25
																	arg:_syncActiveTreadmillSnapshot()
																end

																if arg.activeTreadmill ~= nil and arg:_isTreadmillHudActive() then
																	flag3 = true
																	break
																end

																if arg.activeTreadmill == nil then
																	if n2 <= v75 then
																		n2 = v75 + 0.2

																		local v76, v77, v78 = pcall(function()
																			return v67.Treadmill.AskWearStill:InvokeServer()
																		end)

																		if not v76 or v77 == false then
																			str = tostring(v78 or v77)
																			arg:_setStatus("Preparing carry", "Activation retry")
																		end
																	end
																end

																task.wait(0.03)
																continue
															end

															break
														end
													else
														str = "Positioning failed"
													end
												else
													str = "Position unavailable"
												end

												if flag3 then
													break
												end

												if fn38() then
													return fn40(false, "Cancelled")
												end
												arg:_setStatus("Preparing carry", "Resetting position")
												fn39(1.85)
											end

											if flag3 then
												arg.treadmillEquipPending = false
												arg:_setStatus("Preparing carry", "Active")
												local n = os.clock() + 0.1

												while not fn38() do
													if os.clock() < n then
														task.wait(0.03)
														continue
													end
													break
												end

												if fn38() then
													return fn40(false, "Cancelled")
												end
												arg:_setStatus("Preparing carry", "Releasing")
												if fn39(1.85) then
													return fn40(true)
												end
												local v72 = fn40
												local flag4 = false
												local str2 = fn38() and "Cancelled" or "Release not confirmed"
												return v72(flag4, str2)
											end

											local v72 = fn40
											local flag4 = false
											local v73 = tostring
											local str2 = str or "Unknown"
											return v72(flag4, "Activation not confirmed: " .. v73(str2))
										end

										index.yieldForBackground = function(arg)
											if arg.destroyed then
												return
											end
											arg:_cancelIdle(false)
											if arg.backgroundYieldRunning then
												return
											end
											arg.backgroundYieldRunning = true

											task.spawn(function()
												local n = 0.15

												while not arg.destroyed do
													if arg.coordinator and arg.coordinator:isBackgroundBusy() then
														arg.lastBackgroundYieldAt = os.clock()
														arg:_setStatus("Idle", "Yielding to background inventory")
														arg:_requestUnequip()
														task.wait(n)
														if arg:_syncActiveTreadmillSnapshot() then
															n = math.min(n * 2, 0.75)
															continue
														end
														break
													end

													break
												end

												arg.backgroundYieldRunning = false

												if not arg.destroyed then
													if not arg:isEngaged() then
														arg:_setStatus("Idle", "Background inventory ready")
													end
												end
											end)
										end

										index._startIdle = function(arg)
											if not arg.destroyed and not arg.busy and arg.config.idleFarm then
												arg:_cancelIdle(false)
												local idleGeneration = arg.idleGeneration
												local v72 = os.clock()
												arg.idleRunning = idleGeneration

												task.spawn(function()
													local v73, v74 = xpcall(function()
														while not arg.destroyed do
															if arg.busy then
																break
															end

															if arg.config.idleFarm then
																if idleGeneration == arg.idleGeneration then
																	local n, v73, travel, coordinator, coordinator2, v74, v75, v76, v77, setStatus, str, v78, v79, n2, coordinator3

																	if arg.coordinator then
																		if arg.coordinator:isBackgroundBusy() then
																			v72 = os.clock()
																			arg:_setStatus("Idle", "Waiting for background inventory")
																			task.wait(0.25)
																		elseif fn36() then
																			v72 = os.clock()
																			arg:_setStatus("Idle", "Yielding to Auto Steal")
																			task.wait(0.25)
																		elseif v53.__SenaV4IsAutoSellEggBusy and v53.__SenaV4IsAutoSellEggBusy() then
																			v72 = os.clock()
																			arg:_setStatus("Idle", "Waiting for Auto Sell Egg")
																			task.wait(0.25)
																		elseif fn35() then
																			v72 = os.clock()
																			arg:_setStatus("Idle", fn37())
																			task.wait(0.25)
																		else
																			n = 1 - os.clock() - v72

																			if n > 0 then
																				arg:_setStatus("Idle", string.format("Starting in %.0fs", math.ceil(n)))
																				task.wait(math.min(n, 0.25))
																			else
																				v73 = arg:_resolveStandCFrame()

																				if v73 then
																					arg.travelingToTreadmill = true
																					arg:_setStatus("Moving", "Waiting")

																					travel = arg.travel and arg.travel:travelAndWait(v73, { abort = function()
																						return arg.destroyed or arg.busy or not arg.config.idleFarm or idleGeneration ~= arg.idleGeneration or arg.coordinator and arg.coordinator:isBackgroundBusy() or v53.__SenaV4IsAutoSellEggBusy and v53.__SenaV4IsAutoSellEggBusy() or fn35()
																					end })

																					arg.travelingToTreadmill = false

																					if travel then
																						if not arg.busy then
																							if arg.config.idleFarm then
																								if idleGeneration == arg.idleGeneration then
																									if arg.coordinator then
																										coordinator = arg.coordinator
																										if coordinator:isBackgroundBusy() then
																											continue
																										end
																									end

																									if not v53.__SenaV4IsAutoSellEggBusy or not v53.__SenaV4IsAutoSellEggBusy() then
																										if not fn35() then
																											task.wait(0.35)

																											if not arg.busy then
																												if arg.config.idleFarm then
																													if idleGeneration == arg.idleGeneration then
																														if arg.coordinator then
																															coordinator2 = arg.coordinator
																															if coordinator2:isBackgroundBusy() then
																																continue
																															end
																														end

																														if not v53.__SenaV4IsAutoSellEggBusy or not v53.__SenaV4IsAutoSellEggBusy() then
																															if not fn35() then
																																arg.treadmillEquipPending = true

																																v74, v75, v76 = pcall(function()
																																	return v67.Treadmill.AskWearStill:InvokeServer()
																																end)

																																if v74 and v75 ~= false then
																																	arg.lastEquipAt = os.clock()
																																	arg:_setStatus("Active", "Waiting for gain")

																																	task.delay(2, function()
																																		if not arg.destroyed then
																																			if arg.activeTreadmill == nil then
																																				arg.treadmillEquipPending = false
																																			end
																																		end
																																	end)
																																else
																																	arg.treadmillEquipPending = false
																																	v77 = arg
																																	setStatus = arg._setStatus
																																	str = "Preparing carry"
																																	v78 = tostring
																																	v79 = v76 or v75
																																	setStatus(v77, str, "Activation failed: " .. v78(v79))
																																end

																																n2 = os.clock() + 8

																																while arg.activeTreadmill ~= nil or os.clock() < n2 do
																																	if arg.destroyed then
																																		break
																																	end

																																	if arg.busy then
																																		break
																																	end

																																	if arg.config.idleFarm then
																																		if idleGeneration == arg.idleGeneration then
																																			if arg.coordinator then
																																				coordinator3 = arg.coordinator
																																				if coordinator3:isBackgroundBusy() then
																																					break
																																				end
																																			end

																																			if fn36() then
																																				arg:_cancelIdle(true)
																																				arg:_setStatus("Idle", "Yielding to Auto Steal")
																																				break
																																			end

																																			if v53.__SenaV4IsAutoSellEggBusy and v53.__SenaV4IsAutoSellEggBusy() then
																																				arg:_cancelIdle(true)
																																				arg:_setStatus("Idle", "Yielding to Auto Sell Egg")
																																				break
																																			end

																																			if fn35() then
																																				arg:_requestUnequip()
																																				arg:_setStatus("Idle", fn37())
																																				break
																																			end

																																			task.wait(0.25)
																																			continue
																																		end

																																		break
																																	end

																																	break
																																end
																															end
																														end
																													end
																												end
																											end
																										end
																									end
																								end
																							end
																						end
																					elseif not arg.busy then
																						if arg.config.idleFarm then
																							arg:_setStatus("Idle", "Travel failed")
																							task.wait(1)
																						end
																					end
																				else
																					arg:_setStatus("Idle", "Position unavailable")
																					task.wait(2)
																				end
																			end
																		end
																	elseif fn36() then
																		v72 = os.clock()
																		arg:_setStatus("Idle", "Yielding to Auto Steal")
																		task.wait(0.25)
																	elseif v53.__SenaV4IsAutoSellEggBusy and v53.__SenaV4IsAutoSellEggBusy() then

								local function fn36()
									local character = localPlayer2.Character

													end

														pcall(function()
															descendant:Destroy()
														end)
													end
												end
											end
										end

											humanoid.PlatformStand = false

								if not arg:_isGrabPrompt(arg2) or not arg2.Enabled then
									return
								end
								arg.grabFiring = arg.grabFiring or setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
								if arg.grabFiring[arg2] then
									return
								end
								local parent = arg2.Parent
								local v66 = arg:_root()
								if not v66 or not parent or not parent:IsA("BasePart") then
									return
								end

								if (tonumber(arg2.MaxActivationDistance) or 10) + 1 < (parent.Position - v66.Position).Magnitude then
									return
								end
								arg.grabFiring[arg2] = true

								task.spawn(function()
									pcall(function()
										local v67 = math.max(0.1, tonumber(arg2.HoldDuration) or 0.1)
										arg2:InputHoldBegin()
										task.wait(v67 + 0.05)
										arg2:InputHoldEnd()
									end)

									task.wait(0.4)

									if arg.grabFiring then
										arg.grabFiring[arg2] = nil
									end
								end)
							end

							index.setInstantGrab = function(arg, arg2)
								arg.grabEnabled = arg2 == true

								if arg.grabEnabled then
									for _, descendant in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
										arg:_applyGrab(descendant)
									end

									if not arg.grabConnection then
										arg.grabConnection = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
											if arg.grabEnabled then
												if arg:_isGrabPrompt(descendant) then
													task.defer(function()
														arg:_applyGrab(descendant)
													end)
												end
											end
										end)
									end
								else
									if arg.grabConnection then
										pcall(function()
											arg.grabConnection:Disconnect()
										end)

										arg.grabConnection = nil
									end

									arg.grabFireLoop = nil
									arg:_restoreGrab()
								end
							end

							index.triggerJump = function(arg)
								if arg.jumpEnabled then
									local v66 = os.clock()
									if v66 - arg.lastJumpAt < 0.08 then
										return
									end
									local v67 = arg:_humanoid()
									local v68 = arg:_root()
									if not v67 or not v68 or v67.Health <= 0 then
										return
									end
									arg.lastJumpAt = v66

									pcall(function()
										v67:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, true)
										v67:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
										v67.Jump = true
									end)

									local n = 55

									if v67.UseJumpPower and v67.JumpPower > 0 then
										n = v67.JumpPower
									elseif v67.JumpHeight then
										if v67.JumpHeight > 0 then
											n = math.sqrt(2 * workspace.Gravity * v67.JumpHeight)
										end
									end

									if n < 55 then
										n = 55
									end

									pcall(function()
										local assemblyLinearVelocity = v68.AssemblyLinearVelocity
										v68.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(assemblyLinearVelocity.X, n, assemblyLinearVelocity.Z)
									end)

									return
								end
							end

							index.setInfiniteJump = function(arg, arg2)
								arg.jumpEnabled = arg2 == true
								if #arg.jumpConnections > 0 then
									return
								end

								pcall(function()
									table.insert(arg.jumpConnections, UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
										if arg.jumpEnabled then
											if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
												return
											end

											if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Space or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonA then
												arg:triggerJump()
											end

											return
										end
									end))

									table.insert(arg.jumpConnections, UserInputService.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
										if arg.jumpEnabled then
											if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
												return
											end
											arg:triggerJump()
											return
										end
									end))

										RunService2.Heartbeat:Wait()

											local flag3 = false

											pcall(function()
												if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) or UserInputService:IsGamepadButtonDown(Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonA) then
													flag3 = true
												end
											end)

											if flag3 then
												local lastJumpAt = arg.lastJumpAt

												if os.clock() - lastJumpAt >= 0.28 then
													arg:triggerJump()
												end
											end
										end
									end
								end)
							end

							index._gameWalkSpeed = function(arg)
								local speedModules = arg.speedModules

									end)

									pcall(function()
										speedModules.Save = require(ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Save)
									end)

									arg.speedModules = speedModules
								end

								if not speedModules.TreadmillUtil or not speedModules.TreadmillUtil.SpeedPowerToWalkSpeed then
									return nil
								end
								local speedPower = nil

								if speedModules.Projection then
									pcall(function()
										speedPower = speedModules.Projection.ReadProjected()
									end)
								end

								if type(speedPower) ~= "number" then
									if speedModules.Save then
										pcall(function()
											speedPower = (speedModules.Save.Peek or speedModules.Save.Get)().SpeedPower
										end)
									end
								end

								if type(speedPower) ~= "number" then
									return nil
								end
								local v66, v67 = pcall(speedModules.TreadmillUtil.SpeedPowerToWalkSpeed, speedPower)
								local v68 = v66 and tonumber(v67) or nil
								return v68
							end

							index.setSpeedMultiplier = function(arg, arg2)

										return

										pcall(function()
											v66.WalkSpeed = walkSpeed
										end)
									end

								end
							end

						index._check = function(arg, arg2)

			end

				if type(getconnections) == "function" then
					return getconnections
				end

				if type(get_signal_cons) == "function" then
					return get_signal_cons
				end
				return nil
			end

			local function fn34(arg)
				local localPlayer2 = Players.LocalPlayer

				if localPlayer2 then
					local v58 = fn33()

					if v58 then
						local v59, v60 = pcall(v58, localPlayer2.Idled)
						if not v59 or type(v60) ~= "table" then
							arg.antiAfkStatus = "Idled scan unavailable"
							return false
						end
						local lastDisabledIdleConnections = 0

						for _, v61 in ipairs(v60) do
							local flag3 = false

							if type(v61) == "table" or type(v61) == "userdata" then
								if type(v61.Disable) == "function" then
									flag3 = pcall(v61.Disable, v61)
								elseif type(v61.Disconnect) == "function" then
									flag3 = pcall(v61.Disconnect, v61)
								end
							end

							if flag3 then
								lastDisabledIdleConnections += 1
							end
						end

						arg.scanRuns = arg.scanRuns + 1
						arg.lastScanAt = os.clock()
						arg.lastDisabledIdleConnections = lastDisabledIdleConnections
						arg.disabledIdleConnections = arg.disabledIdleConnections + lastDisabledIdleConnections
						arg.antiAfkStatus = lastDisabledIdleConnections > 0 and "Anti-AFK disabled " .. tostring(lastDisabledIdleConnections) .. " Idled connection(s)" or "Anti-AFK active; Idled connections clear"
						return true
					end

					arg.antiAfkStatus = "Native Idled connection API unavailable"
					return false
				end

				arg.antiAfkStatus = "LocalPlayer unavailable"
				return false
			end

			senaV4StartupProtection = {
				install = function()
					local v58 = fn32()
					local v59 = rawget(v58, "__FyyStealAnEggStartupProtectionLiteV2")

					if type(v59) == "table" and v59.installed then
						v59.installRuns = v59.installRuns + 1
						fn34(v59)
						v59.status = v59.antiAfkStatus
						return v59
					end

					local fyyStealAnEggStartupProtectionLi = {
						installRuns = 1,
						scanRuns = 0,
						disabledIdleConnections = 0,
						lastDisabledIdleConnections = 0,
						lastScanAt = 0,
						installed = false,
						mode = "native-idled-disabler",
						integrityPatcherInstalled = false,
						networkFilterInstalled = false,
						namecallHookInstalled = false,
					}

					v58.__FyyStealAnEggStartupProtectionLiteV2 = fyyStealAnEggStartupProtectionLi
					local localPlayer2 = Players.LocalPlayer

					if localPlayer2 then
						fyyStealAnEggStartupProtectionLi.antiAfkInstalled = fn34(fyyStealAnEggStartupProtectionLi)

						fyyStealAnEggStartupProtectionLi.idledConnection = localPlayer2.Idled:Connect(function()
							fn34(fyyStealAnEggStartupProtectionLi)
						end)

						fyyStealAnEggStartupProtectionLi.characterConnection = localPlayer2.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
							task.delay(1, function()
								if fyyStealAnEggStartupProtectionLi.installed then
									fn34(fyyStealAnEggStartupProtectionLi)
								end
							end)
						end)

						fyyStealAnEggStartupProtectionLi.installed = true
						fyyStealAnEggStartupProtectionLi.status = fyyStealAnEggStartupProtectionLi.antiAfkStatus

						local function fn35()
							while fyyStealAnEggStartupProtectionLi.installed do
								if v58.__FyyStealAnEggStartupProtectionLiteV2 == fyyStealAnEggStartupProtectionLi then
									task.wait(60)

									if fyyStealAnEggStartupProtectionLi.installed then
										if v58.__FyyStealAnEggStartupProtectionLiteV2 == fyyStealAnEggStartupProtectionLi then
											fn34(fyyStealAnEggStartupProtectionLi)
											fyyStealAnEggStartupProtectionLi.status = fyyStealAnEggStartupProtectionLi.antiAfkStatus
										end
									end

									continue
								end

								break
							end
						end

						task.spawn(fn35)
						return fyyStealAnEggStartupProtectionLi
					end

					fyyStealAnEggStartupProtectionLi.antiAfkInstalled = false
					fyyStealAnEggStartupProtectionLi.antiAfkStatus = "LocalPlayer unavailable"
					fyyStealAnEggStartupProtectionLi.status = fyyStealAnEggStartupProtectionLi.antiAfkStatus
					fyyStealAnEggStartupProtectionLi.installed = true
					return fyyStealAnEggStartupProtectionLi
				end,
				uninstall = function()
					local v58 = rawget(fn32(), "__FyyStealAnEggStartupProtectionLiteV2")

					if type(v58) == "table" then
						v58.installed = false

						if v58.idledConnection then
							if typeof(v58.idledConnection.Disconnect) == "function" then
								pcall(v58.idledConnection.Disconnect, v58.idledConnection)
								v58.idledConnection = nil
							end
						end

						if v58.characterConnection then
							if typeof(v58.characterConnection.Disconnect) == "function" then
								pcall(v58.characterConnection.Disconnect, v58.characterConnection)
								v58.characterConnection = nil
							end
						end
					end
				end,
			}
		end

		local function fn32()
			local v58 = getgenv and getgenv() or _G
			v58.__SenaV4AntiAfkGeneration = (v58.__SenaV4AntiAfkGeneration or 0) + 1

				end
			end

			if senaV4Config.AntiAFK then
				v58.__SenaV4SetAntiAfkEnabled(true)
			end
		end

		fn32()
	end

					end

						end)
					end

		end

				local function fn34()
					local tbl4 = { snapAt = 0, snap = nil, status = "Idle", lastBuy = 0, doneLost = {}, active = false, token = 0 }

					local function fn35()
						return require(ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Remotes).Scramble
					end

					tbl4.snapshot = function(arg)
						local v59 = os.clock()
						if not arg and tbl4.snap and v59 - tbl4.snapAt < 1.5 then
							return tbl4.snap
						end

						local v60, v61 = pcall(function()
							return fn35().Request:InvokeServer("Snapshot")
						end)

							if type(v61) == "table" then
								if v61.State then
									tbl4.snap = v61
									tbl4.snapAt = v59
								end
							end

					end

						local v59 = tbl4.snapshot()

						if v59 then
							local flag3 = v59.Ready == true and v59.Enabled == true and v59.WorldReady == true

							if flag3 then
								flag3 = workspace:GetServerTimeNow() < (v59.EventEndsAt or 0)
							end

					end

							return state.Samples or 0, state.TotalParts or 0, state.DroneParts or 0

					tbl4.buyTick = function()
						local scrambleBuyList = senaV4Config.ScrambleBuyList
						if not scrambleBuyList or #scrambleBuyList == 0 then
							return
						end
						local lastBuy = tbl4.lastBuy
						if os.clock() - lastBuy < 2 then
							return
						end
						local v59 = tbl4.snapshot(true)
						if not v59 or not v59.State or not v59.Shop then
							return
						end
						local samples = v59.State.Samples or 0

						for _, v60 in pairs(v59.Shop) do
							if senaV4Config.ScrambleAutoBuy then
								local v61 = tostring(v60.Label)
								local flag3 = false

								for _, v62 in ipairs(scrambleBuyList) do
									if tostring(v62) == v61 then
										flag3 = true
										break
									end
								end

								if flag3 then
									if (v60.Price or 0) <= samples then
										local v62 = (v59.State.ShopPurchases or {})[v60.Id]
										local count = v62 and v62.Period == v59.ShopPeriod and (v62.Count or 0) or 0

										if v60.PurchaseLimit then
											if count + (v60.Quantity or 1) <= v60.PurchaseLimit then
												tbl4.lastBuy = os.clock()

												pcall(function()
													fn35().Request:InvokeServer("Shop", v60.Id, { Quote = v60.Quote, Sequence = v59.State.ShopSequence })
												end)

												tbl4.status = "Bought " .. v61
												tbl4.snapshot(true)
												return
											end

											continue
										end

										tbl4.lastBuy = os.clock()

										pcall(function()
											fn35().Request:InvokeServer("Shop", v60.Id, { Quote = v60.Quote, Sequence = v59.State.ShopSequence })
										end)

										tbl4.status = "Bought " .. v61
										tbl4.snapshot(true)
										return
									end

									continue
								end

								continue
							end

							return
						end
					end

					local function fn38(arg, arg2, ...)

					senaV4ButterflyRuntime.bloomLeft = function()

						if typeof(attribute) ~= "number" then

					senaV4ButterflyRuntime.nextBloom = function()
						local n = tonumber(fn37("IntervalSeconds", 1800)) or 1800
						local n2 = tonumber(fn37("OffsetSeconds", 900)) or 900
						local n3 = tonumber(fn37("DurationSeconds", 300)) or 300
						if n <= 0 then
							return nil
						end
						local serverTimeNow = workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
						local v60 = fn36()
						local attribute = workspace:GetAttribute(v60 and v60.Catalog.EndsAtAttribute or "ButterflyBloomEndsAt")
						local n4 = typeof(attribute) == "number" and attribute - n3 or math.floor((serverTimeNow - n2) / n) * n + n2
						local n5 = 0

						while n4 <= serverTimeNow do
							if n5 < 64 then
								n4 += n
								n5 += 1
								continue
							end

							break
						end

						return n4 > serverTimeNow and n4 - serverTimeNow or nil
					end

					senaV4ButterflyRuntime.smartNext = function(arg)
						local v61 = senaV4ButterflyRuntime.tiers()
						if not v61 or not arg then
							return nil
						end

						for i = 3, 1, -1 do
							local v62 = tbl4[i]
							local v63 = tbl4[i + 1]
							local trade = v61[v62].trade

							if trade > 0 then
								if (arg[v63] or 0) < v61[v63].essence then
									if trade <= (arg[v62] or 0) - v61[v62].essence then
										return v62
									end
								end
							end
						end

						return nil
					end

					senaV4ButterflyRuntime.canTrade = function()

						if butterflyTradeRecipe == "Smart" then
							return senaV4ButterflyRuntime.smartNext(snap.counts) ~= nil
						end

						for i = 1, 3 do
							local v62 = tbl4[i]

							if (butterflyTradeRecipe == nil or butterflyTradeRecipe == "All" or butterflyTradeRecipe == v62) and v61[v62].trade > 0 then
								if v61[v62].trade <= (snap.counts[v62] or 0) then
									return true
								end
							end
						end

					end

					local function fn48(arg, arg2)
						local character = localPlayer.Character
						local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
						if not humanoidRootPart or not humanoid then

							if fn2() and not (n < os.clock()) then
								return tbl6.blocker("butterfly") ~= nil
							end
							return true

								senaV4ButterflyRuntime.status = "Heading to the safe zone first"
								pcall(fn23, humanoidRootPart, humanoid, v61, fn49)
								senaV4ButterflyRuntime.status = status
								humanoidRootPart = fn41()
								if not humanoidRootPart or fn49() or not v55:_isInSafeArea(humanoidRootPart) then
									return false
								end
							end

					local function fn50(arg)
						if not arg or not arg:IsA("ProximityPrompt") or not arg.Enabled then
							return false
						end
						local n = tonumber(arg.HoldDuration) or 0

						pcall(function()
							arg:InputHoldBegin()
							task.wait(n > 0 and n + 0.15 or 0.05)
							arg:InputHoldEnd()
						end)

						if typeof(fireproximityprompt) == "function" then
							pcall(fireproximityprompt, arg, n > 0 and n + 0.15 or 0)
						end

						return true
					end

					local enchantedTreePrompt = nil

					local function fn51()
						if fn49() then
							return nil
						end

						if localPlayer:GetAttribute("EnchantedTreeUnlocked") ~= true then
							return "Enchanted Tree is locked (finish the Wisp Event)"
						end

						if not enchantedTreePrompt or not enchantedTreePrompt.Parent then
							local world = fn35(workspace, "World", "Build", "EnchantedTreeInterior", "Markers", "EnterTrigger")
							enchantedTreePrompt = world and world:FindFirstChild("EnchantedTreePrompt", true) or nil
						end

						local parent = enchantedTreePrompt and enchantedTreePrompt.Parent
						local worldPosition = parent and (parent:IsA("Attachment") and parent.WorldPosition or parent:IsA("BasePart") and parent.Position)

						if worldPosition then
							if fn16() then
								return "night - the forest is sealed"
							end
							senaV4ButterflyRuntime.status = "Flying to the Enchanted Tree"

							if fn48(worldPosition + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0), 60) then
								senaV4ButterflyRuntime.status = "Entering the tree"
								fn50(enchantedTreePrompt)
								local n = os.clock() + 8

								while os.clock() < n do
									if fn49() then
										break
									end
									task.wait(0.05)
								end

								if fn49() then
									return nil
								end
								return "entering the tree failed"
							end

							return "could not reach the tree entrance"
						end

						return "tree entrance not found"
					end

					local function fn52()

					senaV4ButterflyRuntime.tradeUp = function()
						senaV4ButterflyRuntime.snapshot()

						if senaV4ButterflyRuntime.canTrade() then
							local ButterflyMachine, v61 = fn54("ButterflyMachine")

							if ButterflyMachine then
								local v62 = senaV4ButterflyRuntime.tiers()
								local butterflyTradeRecipe = senaV4Config.ButterflyTradeRecipe
								local n = 0

									local v63 = table.clone(senaV4ButterflyRuntime.snap.counts)
									local v64

									while true do
										if n < 50 then
											local v65 = senaV4ButterflyRuntime.smartNext(v63)

													local v66 = tbl4[tbl5[v65] + 1]
													v63[v66] = (v63[v66] or 0) + 1
													task.wait(0.12)
													continue
												end

												break
											end

											return true, n
										else
											return true, n
										end
									end

									if n == 0 then
										return false, v64 or "the machine refused"
									end
									return true, n
								end

								local v63

								for i = 3, 1, -1 do
									local v64 = tbl4[i]

									if butterflyTradeRecipe == nil or butterflyTradeRecipe == "All" or butterflyTradeRecipe == v64 then
										local n2 = senaV4ButterflyRuntime.snap.counts[v64] or 0
										local trade = v62[v64].trade
										local exitTo = nil

										while trade > 0 and trade <= n2 do
											senaV4ButterflyRuntime.status = "Trading up " .. v62[v64].name
											local Butterflies
											Butterflies, v63 = fn38("Butterflies", "AskTradeUp", v64)

											if Butterflies then
												n += 1
												n2 -= trade
												task.wait(0.12)
												continue
											end

											exitTo = 1
											break
										end

										if exitTo == 1 then
											if n == 0 then
												return false, v63 or "the machine refused"
											end
										end
									end
								end

								return true, n
							end

							return false, v61
						end

						return false, "nothing to trade up"
					end

					senaV4ButterflyRuntime.craft = function()

					tbl12.route = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
						if arg == "Circle" then
							local n = arg3 * 2.0833333333333335
							return arg2 + Vector3.new(math.cos(n) * 24, 0, math.sin(n) * 24)
						end
						local v61 = fn55()
						if arg ~= "Zigzag" or not v61 then
							return arg2
						end
						local v62 = math.max(v61.Size.X / 2 - 12, 20)
						local v63 = math.clamp(v61.Size.Z / 2 - 12, 8, 30)
						local n = arg3 * 80 / math.sqrt((2 * v62 / 8) ^ 2 + (2 * v63) ^ 2) % 16

					spawnEgg = function(arg, arg2)

										Mutations = {},
									}
								end)
							end)

							return v64
						end

						return nil
					end,
					spawnPet = function(arg, arg2)
						local v62 = fn32()

						if v62 and v63 and v63[arg] then
							local v64 = fn34("P")

							pcall(function()
								v62.Amend("Inventory", function(arg3)
									arg3[v64] = {
										Category = arg,
										Scale = tonumber(arg2) or 50,
										ColorIndex = 1,
										ColorSeed = math.random(1, 2e9),

										Personality = "Normal",
										InFuse = false,
										HasBeenFirstPlaced = false,
										Mutations = {},
									}
								end)
							end)

							return v64
						end

						return nil
					end,
				}
			end
		end

		do
			local function fn34(arg)
				local assets = ReplicatedStorage2:FindFirstChild("Assets")
				local models = assets and assets:FindFirstChild("Models")
				local eggs = models and models:FindFirstChild("Eggs")
				return eggs and eggs:FindFirstChild(arg)
			end

			local function fn35(arg)
				local assetModels = ReplicatedStorage2:FindFirstChild("AssetModels")
				return assetModels and assetModels:FindFirstChild(arg)
			end

			local function fn36()
				for _, v62 in ipairs(senaV4VisualSAE.held) do
					pcall(function()
						v62:Destroy()
					end)
				end

				senaV4VisualSAE.held = {}

				for _, v62 in ipairs({ localPlayer2.Character, localPlayer2:FindFirstChild("Backpack") }) do
					if v62 then
						for _, child in ipairs(v62:GetChildren()) do
							if child:IsA("Tool") then
