									end)
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			senaV4VisualSAE.hold = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
				local character = localPlayer2.Character

				if character then
					local v62 = arg2 and fn34(arg) or fn35(arg)

					if v62 then
						local v63, v64 = pcall(function()
							return v62:Clone()
						end)

						if not v63 or not v64 or not v64:IsA("Model") then
							return false
						end
						local primaryPart = v64.PrimaryPart or v64:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")

						if primaryPart then
							pcall(function()
								if not v64.PrimaryPart then
									v64.PrimaryPart = primaryPart
								end
							end)

							local n = math.clamp(1 + (tonumber(arg3) or 50) / 70, 1, 2.4) * (0.85 + math.random() * 0.4)

							pcall(function()
								if v64.ScaleTo then
									v64:ScaleTo(n)
								end
							end)

							local extentsSize = Vector3.new(3, 3, 3)

							pcall(function()
								extentsSize = v64:GetExtentsSize()
							end)

							local Tool = Instance.new("Tool")
							Tool.Name = "SENAVIS_" .. arg
							Tool.RequiresHandle = true
							Tool.CanBeDropped = false
							local v65 = fn33()
							local v66 = v65 and v65[arg]
							local icon = v66 and (arg2 and v66.Egg and v66.Egg.Icon or v66.Icon)

							if type(icon) == "string" then
								pcall(function()
									Tool.TextureId = icon
								end)
							end

							primaryPart.Name = "Handle"

							for _, descendant in ipairs(v64:GetDescendants()) do
								if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
									descendant.Massless = true
									descendant.CanCollide = false
									descendant.Anchored = false

									if descendant ~= primaryPart then
										local WeldConstraint = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
										WeldConstraint.Part0 = primaryPart
										WeldConstraint.Part1 = descendant
										WeldConstraint.Parent = primaryPart
									end
								end
							end

							for _, child in ipairs(v64:GetChildren()) do
								child.Parent = Tool
							end

							primaryPart.Parent = Tool

							pcall(function()
								v64:Destroy()
							end)

							pcall(function()
								Tool.Grip = CFrame.new(0, -(extentsSize.Y * 0.55), math.max(extentsSize.X, extentsSize.Z) * 0.5 + 0.5)
							end)

							Tool.Parent = localPlayer2:FindFirstChild("Backpack") or character
							local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

							if humanoid then
								pcall(function()
									humanoid:EquipTool(Tool)
								end)
							end

							senaV4VisualSAE.held[#senaV4VisualSAE.held + 1] = Tool
							return true
						end

						pcall(function()
							v64:Destroy()
						end)

						return false
					end

					return false
				end

				return false
			end
		end

		senaV4VisualSAE.spawn = function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
			if not arg2 or arg2 == "None" then
				return
			end
			local n = tonumber(arg3) or 50

			if arg4 then
				pcall(senaV4VisualSAE.hold, arg2, arg ~= "Pet", n)
			elseif arg == "Pet" then
				senaV4VisualSAE.spawnPet(arg2, math.clamp(1 + n / 25, 1.2, 5) * (0.85 + math.random() * 0.4))
			else
				senaV4VisualSAE.spawnEgg(arg2, n * (0.85 + math.random() * 0.5))
			end
		end

		senaV4VisualSAE.clear = function()
			local v62 = fn32()

			if v62 then
				pcall(function()
					v62.Amend("EggInventory", function(arg)
						for k in pairs(arg) do
							if type(k) == "string" then
								if k:sub(1, 8) == "SENAVIS_" then
									arg[k] = nil
								end
							end
						end
					end)
				end)

				pcall(function()
					v62.Amend("Inventory", function(arg)
						for k in pairs(arg) do
							if type(k) == "string" then
								if k:sub(1, 8) == "SENAVIS_" then
									arg[k] = nil
								end
							end
						end
					end)
				end)

				pcall(function()
					v62.Amend("EquippedAssets", function(arg)
						for i = #arg, 1, -1 do
							local v63 = arg[i]

							if type(v63) == "string" then
								if v63:sub(1, 8) == "SENAVIS_" then
									table.remove(arg, i)
								end
							end
						end
					end)
				end)
			end

			for _, v63 in ipairs(senaV4VisualSAE.held) do
				pcall(function()
					v63:Destroy()
				end)
			end

			senaV4VisualSAE.held = {}

			for _, v63 in ipairs({ localPlayer2.Character, localPlayer2:FindFirstChild("Backpack") }) do
				if v63 then
					for _, child in ipairs(v63:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("Tool") then
							if child.Name:sub(1, 8) == "SENAVIS_" then
								pcall(function()
									child:Destroy()
								end)
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		v53.__SenaV4VisualSAE = senaV4VisualSAE
	end

				pcall(fn32)
			end
		end)
	end

				local function fn39(arg, arg2, arg3)

				local function fn41(arg)
					local clone2 = arg.model:Clone()
					clone2.Name = "SenaVisualBack"

					for _, descendant in ipairs(clone2:GetDescendants()) do
						if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
							descendant.Size = descendant.Size * 0.8

							descendant.C0 = CFrame.new(descendant.C0.Position * 0.8) * rotation
						elseif descendant:IsA("Attachment") then
							if not descendant.Parent:IsA("Bone") then
								descendant.Position = descendant.Position * 0.8
							end
						end
					end

					return clone2, clone2:FindFirstChild("RodRoot", true)
				end

					end

					return nil
				end

				tbl4.collectServers = function(arg)
					local tbl5 = {}
					local str = ""

							if str ~= "" then

							if type(v64) ~= "table" then

												end
											end
										end
									end
								end
							end

							str = v64.nextPageCursor or ""
							if str == "" then
								break
							end
							continue
						end

						break
					end

					table.sort(tbl5, function(arg2, arg3)
						return arg2.playing < arg3.playing
					end)

				end

							end

							end
						end

					end

				end
			end
		end

do

				end

		end

				tbl4.privateStatus = "Waiting until you finish stealing..."

	end

					if (tonumber(v66 and v66.FreeRefreshesRemaining) or 0) <= 0 then

						return v67.AskRefresh:InvokeServer()
					end) then
						senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.state(true)
						return true
					end

					return false, "Reroll failed"
				end

				senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.tick = function()
					local v66 = senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.state(false)

					if v66 then
						if v66.Unlocked == false then
							senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.status = "Dr Scramble Trade-In is locked"
							return
						end
						senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.banner = tostring(v66.BannerDisplayName or v66.BannerId or "Unknown")
						senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.rotation = math.max(0, math.floor(tonumber(v66.SecondsUntilRotation) or 0))
						senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.pity = tonumber(v66.PityCount) or 0
						senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.pityMax = tonumber(v66.PityThreshold) or 100
						senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.free = tonumber(v66.FreeRefreshesRemaining) or 0

							senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.status = "Finishing reward"
							local v67 = fn33()

							pcall(function()
								if v67 then
									if v67.AskFinishReveal then
										v67.AskFinishReveal:InvokeServer()
									end
								end
							end)

							senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.cache = nil
							return
						end

						if fn38(v66) then
							local v67 = fn39(v66)
							senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.total = #v67
							senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.demands = #v67 > 0 and fn37(v67, " / ") or "-"

								return
							end

							senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.stock = fn42(v67, v68, v71)
							senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.ready = #v67 - #v70
							senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.missing = #v70 > 0 and fn37(v70, ", ") or "None"

									senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.status = v72 and "Trade complete" or "Trade: " .. tostring(v73)
								else
									senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.status = "Ready to trade (Auto Trade-In is off)"
								end

								return
							end

							if senaV4Config.ScrambleTradeInReroll and senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.free > 0 then
								local lastReroll = senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.lastReroll

								if os.clock() - lastReroll > 3 then

									senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.status = "Rerolling the recipe"
									senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.reroll()
									return
								end
							end

							senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.status = #v70 > 0 and "Waiting for " .. senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.missing or senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.status
							return
						end

						senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.status = "Waiting for banner (now: " .. senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.banner .. ")"
						senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.demands = "-"
						senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.stock = "-"
						senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.ready = 0
						senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.missing = "None"
						return
					end

					senaV4ScrambleTradeIn.status = "Trade-In state unavailable"
				end

			end

		end
	end

						if v63.Contains(arg, n, arg2, false, 3.5) then
							return true
						end

							if v63.Contains(arg, i / 5 * v64, arg2, false, 3.5) then

				local v64 = fn36()

			local function fn41(arg)
				local v64 = fn36()
				local v65 = fn34()
				if not v64 or not v65 then
					return nil
				end
				local attribute = v65:GetAttribute("Center")
				local n = tonumber(v65:GetAttribute("HubRadius")) or 88
				local y = tonumber(v65:GetAttribute("FloorY")) or v64.Position.Y
				local position = v64.Position

				for _, v66 in ipairs({ 9, 15, 23, 33, 45 }) do
					local v67 = nil
					local v68 = nil

					for i = 0, 11 do
						local n2 = i / 12 * 3.1415926535897931 * 2
						local z = position.Z
						local v69 = Vector3.new(position.X + math.cos(n2) * v66, y + 3, z + math.sin(n2) * v66)

						if typeof(attribute) ~= "Vector3" or Vector3.new(v69.X - attribute.X, 0, v69.Z - attribute.Z).Magnitude <= n + 8 then
							if fn39(v69, arg) then
								local magnitude = (v69 - position).Magnitude

								if not v68 or magnitude < v68 then
									v67 = v69
									v68 = magnitude
								end
							end
						end
					end

					if v67 then
						return v67
					end
				end

				return nil
			end

			local function fn42(arg)
				if fn40(arg) then
					local v64 = fn41(arg)

					if v64 then
						local v65 = fn36()

						if v65 then
							v65.CFrame = CFrame.new(v64)
							v65.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
							senaV4ScrambleBoss.dodges = senaV4ScrambleBoss.dodges + 1
						end

						return true
					end

					return false
				end

				return false
			end

			local n = 0

			local function fn43()
				local v64, v65, v66 = fn36()

				if v66 then
					local senaV4IsBatTool = v53.__SenaV4IsBatTool or function(arg)
						return arg:IsA("Tool") and arg:GetAttribute("IsBat") == true
					end

					for _, child in ipairs(v66:GetChildren()) do
						if senaV4IsBatTool(child) then
							return child
						end
					end

					local backpack = localPlayer2:FindFirstChild("Backpack")

					if backpack and v65 then
						for _, child in ipairs(backpack:GetChildren()) do
							if senaV4IsBatTool(child) then
								pcall(function()
									v65:EquipTool(child)
								end)

								return child
							end
						end
					end

					return nil
				end

				return nil
			end

			local function fn44(arg)
				local v64 = fn43()

				if v64 then
					local serverTimeNow = workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
					if serverTimeNow - n < 0.22 then
						return
					end
					n = serverTimeNow

					pcall(function()
						v64:Activate()
					end)

					local handle = v64:FindFirstChild("Handle") or v64:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)

					if handle then
						if arg then
							if type(firetouchinterest) == "function" then
								pcall(function()
									firetouchinterest(handle, arg, 0)
									task.wait(0.02)
									firetouchinterest(handle, arg, 1)
								end)
							end
						end
					end

					senaV4ScrambleBoss.hits = senaV4ScrambleBoss.hits + 1

					pcall(function()
						local v65 = fn33()

						if v65 then
							if v65.BatSwing then
								v65.BatSwing.Trigger:FireServer(nil, string.format("%d:%d:%d", localPlayer2.UserId, senaV4ScrambleBoss.hits, math.floor(serverTimeNow * 1000)))
							end
						end
					end)

					return
				end
			end

			local tbl6 = {
				Floor = true,
				Structure = true,
				Collision = true,
				Windows = true,
				BackgroundCrates = true,
				Crates = true,
				Cranes = true,
				VFX = true,
				Coils = true,
				Center = true,
				BossSpawn = true,
				PlayerSpawn = true,
				LeaveTeleport = true,
				HubBlocker = true,
				FloorSlime = true,
				Drones = true,
				ScrambleHazards = true,
			}

			local function fn45(arg)
				if arg:IsA("BasePart") then
					return arg
				end
				return arg.PrimaryPart or arg:FindFirstChild("Core") or arg:FindFirstChild("Hitbox") or arg:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or arg:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
			end

			local function fn46(arg)
				local v64 = fn36()

				if v64 then
					local v65 = nil
					local v66 = nil
					local v67 = nil

					local function fn47(arg2, arg3)
						local v68 = fn45(arg2)

						if v68 then
							local magnitude = (v68.Position - v64.Position).Magnitude

							if v66 and not (arg3 < v66) then
								if arg3 == v66 then
									if magnitude < v67 then
										v65 = v68
										v66 = arg3
										v67 = magnitude
									end
								end
							else
								v65 = v68
								v66 = arg3
								v67 = magnitude
								return
							end

							return
						end
					end

					for _, v68 in ipairs({ "Mech", "Ball", "Croc", "Human", "DrScramble", "Pilot" }) do
						local v69 = arg:FindFirstChild(v68)

						if v69 then
							fn47(v69, 1)
						end
					end

					for _, child in ipairs(arg:GetChildren()) do
						if not tbl6[child.Name] then
							if child:IsA("Model") then
								if child.Name ~= "Mech" then
									if child:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
										fn47(child, 1)
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end

					local drones = arg:FindFirstChild("Drones")

					if drones then
						for _, child in ipairs(drones:GetChildren()) do
							if child:IsA("Model") then
								local hitbox = child:FindFirstChild("Hitbox") or child.PrimaryPart

								if hitbox then
									fn47(hitbox, 2)
								end
							end
						end
					end

					return v65
				end

				return nil
			end

			local function fn47()
				if localPlayer2:GetAttribute("ScrambleGrabbed") == true then
					pcall(function()
						local v64 = fn33()

						if v64 then
							if v64.ScrambleBoss then
								v64.ScrambleBoss.HazardHit:FireServer(-2)
							end
						end
					end)

					return true
				end

				return false
			end

			local function fn48(arg, arg2)
				local v64 = fn36()

				if v64 then
					local n2 = arg - v64.Position
					local magnitude = n2.Magnitude
					if magnitude < 0.5 then
						return
					end
					v64.CFrame = CFrame.new(v64.Position + n2.Unit * math.min(magnitude, arg2 or 40))
					v64.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
					return
				end
			end

			local function fn49(arg)
				local v64 = fn36()

				if v64 then
					local position = v64.Position
					local v65 = math.max(1, math.ceil((arg - position).Magnitude / 140))

					for i = 1, v65 do
						local v66 = fn36()

						if v66 then
							v66.CFrame = CFrame.new(position:Lerp(arg, i / v65))
							v66.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
							task.wait(0.08)
							continue
						end

						return
					end

					return
				end
			end

			local function leaveArena()
				local v64 = fn34()

				if v64 then
					local v65 = fn36()

					if v65 then
						local leaveTeleport = v64:FindFirstChild("LeaveTeleport")
						local isBasePart = leaveTeleport and (leaveTeleport:IsA("BasePart") and leaveTeleport or leaveTeleport:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true))

						if isBasePart then
							v65.CFrame = CFrame.new(isBasePart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
							v65.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
							return true
						end

						return false
					end

					return false
				end

				return false
			end

			senaV4ScrambleBoss.leaveArena = leaveArena
			local n2 = 0

			task.spawn(function()
				while fn2() do
					if senaV4Config.ScrambleBossAuto and not fn37() then
						local v64 = fn34()

						if amInArena() then
							senaV4ScrambleBoss.inArena = true
							local serverTimeNow = workspace:GetServerTimeNow()

							if fn42(serverTimeNow) then
								senaV4ScrambleBoss.status = "Dodging"
							elseif fn47() then
								senaV4ScrambleBoss.status = "Breaking free"
							else
								fn43()
								local v65 = fn46(v64)

								if v65 then
									local v66 = fn36()

									if v66 then
										if (v66.Position - v65.Position).Magnitude > 12 then
											fn48(v65.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0), 42)
										end
									end

									fn44(v65)
									senaV4ScrambleBoss.status = "Fighting (" .. tostring(v64:GetAttribute("Phase")) .. ")"
								else
									senaV4ScrambleBoss.status = "In arena (" .. tostring(v64:GetAttribute("Phase")) .. ")"
								end
							end

							task.wait(0.06)
						elseif fn35() then
							senaV4ScrambleBoss.status = "Boss up - joining"
							local v65 = fn35()
							local position = v65 and (v65:IsA("Model") and select(1, v65:GetPivot()).Position or v65.Position)

							if position then
								local v66 = fn36()

								if v66 then
									if v55 then
										if (v66.Position - position).Magnitude > 600 then
											if not v55:_isInSafeArea(v66) then
												local v67 = v55:_resolveSafeCFrame(v66)

												if v67 then
													senaV4ScrambleBoss.status = "Heading to the safe zone first"
													fn49(v67.Position)
												end
											end
										end
									end
								end

								fn49(position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
								senaV4ScrambleBoss.joins = senaV4ScrambleBoss.joins + 1
							end

							task.wait(1.2)
						else
							senaV4ScrambleBoss.inArena = false
							senaV4ScrambleBoss.status = "Waiting for boss"
							task.wait(0.4)
						end
					else
						if senaV4Config.ScrambleBossAuto and fn37() then
							senaV4ScrambleBoss.status = "Yielding to Auto Steal"

							if amInArena() then
								if os.clock() - n2 > 3 then
									n2 = os.clock()
									leaveArena()
								end
							end
						elseif senaV4ScrambleBoss.status ~= "Disabled" then
							senaV4ScrambleBoss.status = "Disabled"
						end

						task.wait(0.3)
					end
				end
			end)

			task.spawn(function()
				while fn2() do
					if senaV4Config.AutoSteal then
						if not senaV4Config.ScrambleBossAuto then
							if amInArena() then
								leaveArena()
								task.wait(0.5)
							end
						end
					end

					task.wait(0.25)
				end
			end)

			v53.__SenaV4ScrambleBoss = senaV4ScrambleBoss
		end

		fn32()
	end
end

local senaPanels

do
	senaPanels = {}

	do
		local function fn32()
			game:GetService("UserInputService")
			local v63 = require(ReplicatedStorage.Data.Assets)
			local v64 = require(ReplicatedStorage.Client.EggState)
			local v65 = require(ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Save)

			local tbl5 = {

			local v66 = tbl5

			local function fn33()
				pcall(function()
					if type(setthreadidentity) == "function" then
						setthreadidentity(8)
					elseif v53 then
						if type(v53.__SenaV4IdentitySetter) == "function" then
							v53.__SenaV4IdentitySetter(8)
						end
					end
				end)
			end

			local function fn34()
				local playerGui = localPlayer and localPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
				if playerGui then
					return playerGui
				end

				local v67, v68 = pcall(function()
					return game:GetService("CoreGui")
				end)

				if v67 and v68 then
					return v68
				end
				return game:GetService("CoreGui")
			end

			local function fn35(arg)
				local n = tonumber(arg) or 0
				if n >= 1e12 then
					return string.format("%.2fT", n / 1e12)
				end

				if n >= 1e9 then
					return string.format("%.2fB", n / 1e9)
				end

				if n >= 1000000 then
					return string.format("%.2fM", n / 1000000)
				end

				if n >= 1000 then
					return string.format("%.2fK", n / 1000)
				end
				return string.format("%d", n)
			end

			local function fn36(arg)
				return fn35(arg)
			end

			local function fn37(arg)
				if typeof(arg) == "Color3" then
					local n = arg.B * 255
					return string.format("#%02x%02x%02x", math.floor(arg.R * 255), math.floor(arg.G * 255), math.floor(n))
				end
				return "#ffffff"
			end

			local function fn38(arg)
				local directory = v63.Directory and v63.Directory[arg]
				if not directory or not directory.Rarity then
					return 0, "?", nil
				end
				local displayName = directory.Rarity.DisplayName or directory.Rarity._id or "?"
				local color = directory.Rarity.Color
				return v54.rarityRank(displayName), displayName, color
			end

			local function fn39(arg)
				local directory = v63.Directory and v63.Directory[arg]
				if type(directory) ~= "table" then
					return nil
				end

				local function fn40(arg2)
					if type(arg2) == "string" and arg2 ~= "" then
						if arg2:find("rbxassetid") or arg2:find("rbxthumb") then
							return arg2
						end

						if arg2:match("^%d+$") then
							return "rbxassetid://" .. arg2
						end
					elseif type(arg2) == "number" and arg2 > 0 then
						return "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(arg2)
					end

					return nil
				end

				local tbl6 = { "Image", "Icon", "Thumbnail", "ImageId", "IconId", "Texture", "Asset", "DisplayImage" }

				for _, v67 in ipairs(tbl6) do
					local v68 = fn40(directory[v67])
					if v68 then
						return v68
					end
				end

				if type(directory.Egg) == "table" then
					for _, v67 in ipairs(tbl6) do
						local v68 = fn40(directory.Egg[v67])
						if v68 then
							return v68
						end
					end
				end

				return nil
			end

			local tbl6 = {}
			local tbl7 = {}
			local v67 = pairs
			local directory = v63.Directory or {}

			for k, v68 in v67(directory) do
				if type(v68) == "table" then
					if v68.Rarity then
						local displayName = v68.Rarity.DisplayName or v68.Rarity._id or "?"
						local v69 = v54.rarityRank(displayName)
						tbl7[displayName] = v69
						table.insert(tbl6, { cat = k, rnum = v69, rname = displayName })
					end
				end
			end

			table.sort(tbl6, function(arg, arg2)
				if arg.rnum == arg2.rnum then
					return arg.cat < arg2.cat
				end
				return arg.rnum > arg2.rnum
			end)

			local function fn40()
				local tbl8 = {}
				local tbl9 = {}

				for _, v68 in ipairs(tbl6) do
					if not tbl8[v68.rname] then
						tbl8[v68.rname] = true
						table.insert(tbl9, { v68.rname, v68.rnum })
					end
				end

				table.sort(tbl9, function(arg, arg2)
					return arg[2] > arg2[2]
				end)

				local tbl10 = { "Any" }

				for _, v68 in ipairs(tbl9) do
					table.insert(tbl10, v68[1])
				end

				return tbl10
			end

			local function fn41()
				local tbl8 = {}

				for _, v68 in ipairs(fn40()) do
					if v68 ~= "Any" then
						table.insert(tbl8, v68)
					end
				end

				return tbl8
			end

			local function fn42(arg)
				local n = 0

				pcall(function()
					n = senaV4Config:getAreaEggValue(arg) or 0
				end)

				if n > 0 then
					return n
				end

				pcall(function()
					local v68 = require(ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Util.AssetEarnings)

					if v68 then
						if type(v68.LiveRatePerSecond) == "function" then
							n = v68.LiveRatePerSecond(arg) or 0
						end
					end
				end)

				local n2 = n or 0
				return n2
			end

			local function fn43(arg)
				local n = 0

				pcall(function()
					local v68 = require(ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Util.EggRecords)
					local assetCategory = arg.AssetCategory or arg.Category
					n = v68.WeightKgForScale(assetCategory, arg.AssetScale or arg.Scale or 1)
				end)

				local n2 = n or 0
				return n2
			end

			local function fn44()
				local n = 0
				local n2 = 0

				pcall(function()
					local v68 = pairs
					local tbl8 = v64.ReadOwnerEggs(localPlayer.UserId) or {}

					for _, v69 in v68(tbl8) do
						if v69.Placement ~= nil then
							n += 1
						else
							n2 += 1
						end
					end
				end)

				return n, math.max(0, 50 - n), n2
			end

			local tbl8 = {}

			local function fn45(arg)
				local assetCategory = arg and (arg.AssetCategory or arg.Category)

				if assetCategory then
					local tbl9 = tbl8[assetCategory]

					if not tbl9 then
						local v68, v69, v70 = fn38(assetCategory)
						tbl9 = { name = assetCategory, rarity = v69, rnum = v68, color = v70, count = 0, last = 0, image = fn39(assetCategory) }
						tbl8[assetCategory] = tbl9
					end

					tbl9.count = tbl9.count + 1
					tbl9.last = os.clock()
					return
				end
			end

			local windows = {}

			local function fn46(arg)
				local v68 = windows[arg]
				return v68 ~= nil and v68.gui ~= nil and v68.gui.Parent ~= nil and v68.gui.Enabled or false
			end

			local fn47 = nil
			local fn48 = nil
			local fn49 = nil

			local tbl9 = {
				list = nil,
				headLbl = nil,
				countLbl = nil,
				statusBtn = nil,
				sel = nil,
				rows = {},
				picked = {},
				chips = {},
				sortBtns = {},
				modeBtns = {},
			}

			local tbl10 = {
				history = {},
				reportedUids = {},
				knownFieldUids = {},
				lastAlert = nil,
				lastReportTime = 0,

				onlineCount = 1,
				category = "targets",
				filter = "All",
			}

			local function fn50(arg)
				fn33()

				if arg == "logs" then
					if fn47 then
						fn47()
					end
				elseif arg == "eggs" then
					if fn48 then
						fn48()
					end
				elseif arg == "inventory" then
					if fn49 then
						fn49()
					end
				elseif arg == "checker" then

						end
					end

					if tbl10 then
						if tbl10.refresh then
							tbl10.refresh()
						end
					end
				end
			end

			local v68 = nil
			local TextLabel = nil
			local str = "rarity"
			local tbl11 = {}

			local function fn51()
				fn33()

				for k, v69 in pairs(tbl11) do
					local flag3 = k == str
					v69.BackgroundColor3 = flag3 and v66.accent or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
					v69.BackgroundTransparency = flag3 and 0.15 or 0.88
					v69.TextColor3 = flag3 and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or v66.btnText

					end
				end
			end

			fn47 = function()
				fn33()

				if v68 then
					for _, child in ipairs(v68:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("Frame") then
							child:Destroy()
						end
					end

					local tbl12 = {}
					local n = 0

					for _, v69 in pairs(tbl8) do
						table.insert(tbl12, v69)
						n += v69.count
					end

					if str == "count" then
						table.sort(tbl12, function(arg, arg2)
							if arg.count == arg2.count then
								return (arg.rnum or 0) > (arg2.rnum or 0)
							end
							return arg.count > arg2.count
						end)
					elseif str == "name" then
						table.sort(tbl12, function(arg, arg2)
							return tostring(arg.name) < tostring(arg2.name)
						end)
					else
						table.sort(tbl12, function(arg, arg2)
							if arg.rnum == arg2.rnum then
								return arg.count > arg2.count
							end
							return (arg.rnum or 0) > (arg2.rnum or 0)
						end)
					end

					for k, v69 in ipairs(tbl12) do
						local Frame = Instance.new("Frame")
						Frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 34)
						Frame.BackgroundColor3 = v66.surface
						Frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.12
						Frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
						Frame.LayoutOrder = k
						Frame.Parent = v68
						Instance.new("UICorner", Frame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
						local UIStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", Frame)
						UIStroke.Color = v66.strokeCol
						UIStroke.Thickness = 1
						UIStroke.Transparency = 0.72
						local Frame2 = Instance.new("Frame")
						Frame2.Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, -10)
						Frame2.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 5)
						Frame2.BorderSizePixel = 0

							Frame3.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
							Frame3.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
							Frame3.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(9, 9)

			tbl9.scan = function()
				local v71 = nil

				pcall(function()
					v71 = v64.ReadFieldEggs()
				end)

				local tbl16 = {}
				local v72 = ipairs
				local records = v71 and v71.Records or {}

				for _, record in v72(records) do
					if record.State ~= "Claimed" then
						if record.State ~= "Carried" then
							if not record.CarrierUserId then
								if record.BottomCFrame then
									if not tostring(record.Uid):find("FirstAreaEgg") then
										local assetCategory = record.AssetCategory
										local directory2 = assetCategory and v63.Directory and v63.Directory[assetCategory]
										local v73, v74, v75 = fn38(assetCategory)

										local v76 = fn42({
											AssetCategory = assetCategory,
											Category = assetCategory,
											AssetScale = record.AssetScale or record.Scale or 1,
											Scale = record.AssetScale or record.Scale or 1,
											Mutations = record.Mutations or {},
										})

										local v77 = fn43(record)
										local n = #tbl16 + 1
										local tbl17 = { rec = record, uid = record.Uid }
										local displayName = directory2 and directory2.DisplayName

										if not displayName then
											displayName = tostring(assetCategory or "Egg")
										end

										tbl17.name = displayName
										tbl17.rarity = v74
										tbl17.rnum = v73
										tbl17.color = v75
										tbl17.area = tostring(record.AreaId or "?")
										tbl17.rate = v76
										tbl17.kg = v77
										tbl17.icon = fn39(assetCategory)
										tbl16[n] = tbl17
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end

				if tbl9.rarity ~= "All" then
					local tbl17 = {}

					for _, v73 in ipairs(tbl16) do
						if v73.rarity == tbl9.rarity then
							tbl17[#tbl17 + 1] = v73
						end
					end

					tbl16 = tbl17
				end

				table.sort(tbl16, function(arg, arg2)
					if tbl9.sort == "rarity" then
						if arg.rnum ~= arg2.rnum then
							return (arg.rnum or 0) > (arg2.rnum or 0)
						end
						return (arg.rate or 0) > (arg2.rate or 0)
					end

					if arg.rate ~= arg2.rate then
						return (arg.rate or 0) > (arg2.rate or 0)
					end
					return (arg.rnum or 0) > (arg2.rnum or 0)
				end)

				return tbl16
			end

					if uiStroke then
						uiStroke.Transparency = flag4 and 0 or 0.65
					end
				end

				for k, sortBtn in pairs(tbl9.sortBtns) do
					local flag4 = k == tbl9.sort
					sortBtn.BackgroundColor3 = flag4 and v66.accent or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
					sortBtn.BackgroundTransparency = flag4 and 0.12 or 0.9
					sortBtn.TextColor3 = flag4 and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or v66.btnText
				end

				for k, modeBtn in v71(modeBtns) do

			end

			tbl9.paintRows = function()
				local n = 0

				for _, row in ipairs(tbl9.rows) do
					local flag4 = tbl9.picked[tostring(row.uid)] == true

					if flag4 then
						n += 1
					end

					row.frame.BackgroundTransparency = flag4 and 0.02 or 0.14
					local uiStroke = row.frame:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")

					if uiStroke then
						uiStroke.Transparency = flag4 and 0 or 0.78
					end

					if row.tick then
						row.tick.BackgroundTransparency = flag4 and 0.05 or 0.86
						row.tick.Text = flag4 and "OK" or ""
					end
				end

				if tbl9.statusBtn then
					if not tbl9.running then
						tbl9.statusBtn.Text = n > 0 and "Steal Selected (" .. n .. ")" or "Steal Selected"
					end
				end

				if tbl9.headLbl then
					tbl9.headLbl.Text = n > 0 and n .. " picked" or "Tap eggs to pick them"
				end
			end

			local function fn58(arg)
				if not arg or type(arg) ~= "string" then
					return "unknown"
				end
				local str5 = arg:lower()
				if str5:find("kong") or str5:find("gorilla") then
					return "gorillaking"
				end

				if str5:find("phoenix") then
					return "phoenix"
				end

				if str5:find("lunar") or str5:find("dragon.-lunar") then
					return "lunardragon"
				end

				if str5:find("mosa") then
					return "mosasaurus"
				end

				if str5:find("tiger") or str5:find("oni") then
					return "onitiger"
				end

				if str5:find("kitsune") then
					return "kitsune"
				end

				if str5:find("unicorn") then
					return "unicorn"
				end

				if str5:find("godzilla") then
					return "godzilla"
				end

				if str5:find("pegasus") then
					return "pegasus"
				end

				if str5:find("archangel") then
					return "archangel"
				end

				if str5:find("ice.-dragon") then
					return "icedragon"
				end

				if str5:find("lava.-dragon") or str5:find("dragon") then
					return "lavadragon"
				end

				if str5:find("cosmic.-dragon") or str5:find("ember") then
					return "cosmicdragon"
				end

				if str5:find("trex") or str5:find("tyranno") or str5:find("t%-rex") then
					return "trex"
				end

				if str5:find("gargoyle") then
					return "gargoyle"
				end

				if str5:find("cerberus") then
					return "cerberus"
				end

				if str5:find("yeti") then
					return "yeti"
				end

				if str5:find("dreadscale") then
					return "dreadscale"
				end

				if str5:find("el.-maja") then
					return "elmaja"
				end
				return (str5:gsub("eternal", ""):gsub("ascended", ""):gsub("vermilion", ""):gsub("divine", ""):gsub("secret", ""):gsub("egg", ""):gsub("[^%a%d]", ""))
			end

			local function fn59(arg)
				if arg and not (arg <= 0) then
					return math.floor((arg + 150) / 300) * 300
				end
				return 0
			end

			local function fn60(arg, arg2)
				if not arg or arg <= 0 then
					return "--:--"
				end
				local v71 = os.time()
				local v72 = os.date("*t", v71)
				local v73 = os.date("*t", arg)
				local str5

				if v73.year == v72.year and v73.yday == v72.yday then
					str5 = "Today"
				elseif v73.year == v72.year and v73.yday == v72.yday - 1 then
					str5 = "Yesterday"
				elseif v73.year == v72.year and v73.yday == v72.yday + 1 then
					str5 = "Tomorrow"
				else
					str5 = string.format("%02d/%02d", v73.month, v73.day)
				end

				if arg2 then
					return string.format("%s %02d:%02d:%02d", str5, v73.hour, v73.min, v73.sec)
				end
				return string.format("%s %02d:%02d", str5, v73.hour, v73.min)
			end

			local function fn61(arg)
				if not arg or arg <= 0 then
					return ""
				end
				local n = os.time() - arg

				if n < 0 then
					local n2 = -n
					if n2 < 60 then
						return "in <1m"
					end

					if n2 < 3600 then
						return string.format("in %dm", math.floor(n2 / 60))
					end
					return string.format("in %.1fh", n2 / 3600)
				end

				if n < 60 then
					return "Just now"
				end

				if n < 3600 then
					return string.format("%dm ago", math.floor(n / 60))
				end

				if n < 86400 then
					return string.format("%.1fh ago", n / 3600)
				end
				return string.format("%.1fd ago", n / 86400)
			end

			local function fn62(arg, arg2)
				local v71 = fn59(arg)
				local v72 = fn59(arg2)
				local v73 = os.time()
				local v74 = os.date("*t", v73)
				local v75 = os.date("*t", v71)
				local v76 = os.date("*t", v72)

				local function fn63(arg3)
					if arg3.year == v74.year and arg3.yday == v74.yday then
						return "Today"
					end

					if arg3.year == v74.year and arg3.yday == v74.yday + 1 then
						return "Tomorrow"
					end

					if arg3.year == v74.year and arg3.yday == v74.yday - 1 then
						return "Yesterday"
					end
					return string.format("%02d/%02d", arg3.month, arg3.day)
				end

				local v77 = fn63(v75)
				local v78 = fn63(v76)
				if v77 == v78 then
					return string.format("%s %02d:%02d - %02d:%02d", v77, v75.hour, v75.min, v76.hour, v76.min)
				end
				return string.format("%s %02d:%02d - %s %02d:%02d", v77, v75.hour, v75.min, v78, v76.hour, v76.min)
			end

			local function fn63(arg)
				if arg then
					if tbl16[arg] then
						return tbl16[arg]
					end

					if tbl17[arg] ~= nil then
						return tbl17[arg]
					end
					tbl17[arg] = fn39(arg) or ""
					return tbl17[arg]
				end

				return ""
			end

			local function fn64(arg)
				local request_ = syn and syn.request or http and http.request or request or http_request

				if request_ then
					local v71 = nil
					if pcall(function()
						v71 = request_(arg)
					end) and v71 then
						return v71
					end
				end

				if arg.Method == "GET" and game and game.HttpGet then
					local response = nil
					if pcall(function()
						response = game:HttpGet(arg.Url, true)
					end) and response then
						return { StatusCode = 200, Body = response }
					end
				end

				return nil
			end

			local function fn65(lastEternal)
				if not lastEternal or not lastEternal.name then
					return false
				end
				local v71 = tonumber(lastEternal.ts) or os.time()
				lastEternal.ts = v71
				local str5 = (lastEternal.rarity or ""):lower()
				if str5 ~= "eternal" and str5 ~= "divine" then
					return false
				end
				local v72 = fn58(lastEternal.name)

				for _, v73 in ipairs(tbl10.history) do
					if v73.uid and lastEternal.uid and v73.uid == lastEternal.uid then
						return false
					end

					if fn58(v73.name) == v72 then
						if math.abs((v73.ts or 0) - v71) < 600 then
							return false
						end
					end
				end

				table.insert(tbl10.history, lastEternal)

				table.sort(tbl10.history, function(arg, arg2)
					return (arg.ts or 0) > (arg2.ts or 0)
				end)

				if str5 == "eternal" then
					if tbl10.lastEternal then
						if (tbl10.lastEternal.ts or 0) <= v71 then
							tbl10.lastEternal = lastEternal
						end
					else
						tbl10.lastEternal = lastEternal
						return true
					end
				elseif str5 == "divine" then
					if tbl10.lastDivine then
						if (tbl10.lastDivine.ts or 0) <= v71 then
							tbl10.lastDivine = lastEternal
						end
					else
						tbl10.lastDivine = lastEternal
						return true
					end
				end

				return true
			end

			tbl10.getLatestSpawn = function(arg, arg2)
				for _, v71 in ipairs(tbl10.history) do
					if not arg or v71.rarity and v71.rarity:lower() == arg:lower() then
						if not arg2 or not (#arg2 > 0) then
							return v71
						end
						local v72 = fn58(v71.name)

						for _, v73 in ipairs(arg2) do
							if fn58(v73.name) == v72 then
								return v71
							end
						end
					end
				end

				return nil
			end

			local function fn66(arg)
				if not arg or type(arg) ~= "string" then
					return nil
				end

				if arg:find("spawned in") then
					local str5 = arg:gsub("<[^>]+>", ""):gsub("[%c]", "")
					local match2, match3, v71 = str5:match("A%s+(%a+)%s+(.-)%s+Egg%s+spawned%s+in%s+([^!]+)")

					if not match2 or not match3 or not v71 then
						match3, match2, v71 = str5:match("([%w%s]+)%s*%((%a+)%)%s+has%s+spawned%s+in%s+([^!]+)")
					end

					if match2 and match3 and v71 then
						local str6 = v71:gsub("%s+$", ""):gsub("^%s+", "")
						local str7 = match3:gsub("%s+$", ""):gsub("^%s+", "")
						return match2:gsub("%s+$", ""):gsub("^%s+", ""), str7, str6
					end

					return nil
				end

				return nil
			end

			local function fn67(arg)
				local v71, v72, v73 = fn66(arg)
				if not v71 or not v72 or not v73 then
					return
				end
				local v74 = os.time()
				local v75 = fn58(v72)
				local v76 = math.floor(v74 / 300)
				local v77 = string.format("alert_%s_%s_%d", v75, v73:lower(), v76)
				if tbl10.reportedUids[v77] then
					return
				end
				tbl10.reportedUids[v77] = v74
				tbl10.lastAlert = { pet = v72, area = v73, rarity = v71, ts = v74, norm = v75 }

				if fn65({ uid = v77, name = v72, area = v73, rarity = v71, ts = v74, reporter = "Server Alert" }) then
					pcall(tbl10.paint)
				end
			end

			tbl10.reportSpawn = function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4, arg5)
				if not arg or tbl10.reportedUids[arg] then
					return
				end
				local v71 = os.time()
				local v72 = tonumber(arg5) or v71
				if v71 - tbl10.lastReportTime < 3 then
					return
				end
				tbl10.reportedUids[arg] = v72
				tbl10.lastReportTime = v71
				local name = localPlayer and localPlayer.Name or "Player"

				if fn65({ uid = tostring(arg), name = arg2, area = arg3, rarity = arg4, ts = v72, reporter = name }) then
					pcall(tbl10.paint)
				end
			end

			tbl10.syncBackend = function()
				local v71 = os.time()
				if v71 - tbl10.lastSync < 10 then
					return
				end
				tbl10.lastSync = v71

				task.spawn(function()
					local senaBackend = getgenv and getgenv().SenaBackend or {}
					local api = senaBackend.Api or "http://56.10.112.179:8080"
					local str5 = senaBackend.Key or ""
					local str6 = localPlayer and tostring(localPlayer.UserId) or "0"

					local v72 = fn64({
						Url = api .. "/egg/latest?key=" .. tostring(str5) .. "&uid=" .. tostring(str6) .. "&t=" .. tostring(v71),
						Method = "GET",
					})

					if v72 then
						if v72.StatusCode == 200 or v72.Status == 200 then
							if v72.Body then
								local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")

								local v73, v74 = pcall(function()
									return HttpService:JSONDecode(v72.Body)
								end)

								if v73 then
									if type(v74) == "table" then
										if v74.ok then
											if v74.last_eternal then
												if type(v74.last_eternal) == "table" then
													if v74.last_eternal.name then
														fn65(v74.last_eternal)
													end
												end
											end

											if v74.last_divine then
												if type(v74.last_divine) == "table" then
													if v74.last_divine.name then
														fn65(v74.last_divine)
													end
												end
											end

											if type(v74.recent_spawns) == "table" then
												for _, recentSpawn in ipairs(v74.recent_spawns) do
													fn65(recentSpawn)
												end
											end

											pcall(tbl10.paint)
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end)
			end

			tbl10.scanField = function()
				local v71 = nil

				pcall(function()
					v71 = v64.ReadFieldEggs()
				end)

				if not v71 or not v71.Records then
					return
				end
				local v72 = os.time()

				if tbl10._fieldInitialized then
					for _, record in ipairs(v71.Records) do
						if record.Uid then
							if not tbl10.knownFieldUids[record.Uid] then
								tbl10.knownFieldUids[record.Uid] = true

								if record.State ~= "Claimed" then
									if record.BottomCFrame then
										local assetCategory = record.AssetCategory
										local v73, v74 = fn38(assetCategory)

										if v74 == "Divine" or v74 == "Eternal" then
											local str5 = assetCategory == "King Kong" and "Gorilla King" or assetCategory == "Ascended Vermilion Phoenix" and "Phoenix" or assetCategory

												if v72 - (tbl10.lastAlert.ts or 0) <= 60 then
													if fn58(tbl10.lastAlert.pet) == fn58(str5) then
														if tbl10.lastAlert.area:lower() == (record.AreaId or ""):lower() then
															ts = tbl10.lastAlert.ts
														end
													end
												end
											end

											tbl10.reportSpawn(record.Uid, str5, record.AreaId or "Field", v74, ts)
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end

					return
				end

				tbl10._fieldInitialized = true

				for _, record in ipairs(v71.Records) do
					if record.Uid then
						tbl10.knownFieldUids[record.Uid] = true
					end
				end

					local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")

					local function fn70(arg2)
						local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera
						local viewportSize = currentCamera and currentCamera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1024, 768)
						if viewportSize.X <= 0 or viewportSize.Y <= 0 then
							return
						end
						local flag4 = UserInputService.TouchEnabled and not UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled or viewportSize.Y < 550
						local n2 = math.min(1, viewportSize.X * (flag4 and 0.86 or 0.94) / v71.w, viewportSize.Y * (flag4 and 0.88 or 0.92) / v71.h)

						if flag4 then
							if n2 > 0.82 then
								n2 = 0.82
							end
						end

						UIScale.Scale = math.clamp(n2, 0.48, 1)
						local scale = UIScale.Scale
						local n3 = v71.w * scale
						local n4 = v71.h * scale

						if arg2 then
							if flag4 then
								local n5 = viewportSize.X - math.floor(n3 / 2) - 14
								local v73 = math.floor(viewportSize.Y / 2)
								Frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(n5, v73)
							else
								local n5 = viewportSize.Y / 2 + n * 20
								Frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(math.floor(viewportSize.X / 2 + n * 24), math.floor(n5))
							end
						end

						local n5 = n3 / 2
						local n6 = n4 / 2
						local v73 = math.min(n5 + 8, viewportSize.X / 2)
						local v74 = math.max(viewportSize.X - n5 - 8, viewportSize.X / 2)
						local v75 = math.min(n6 + 8, viewportSize.Y / 2)
						local v76 = math.max(viewportSize.Y - n6 - 8, viewportSize.Y / 2)
						local n7 = Frame.Position.Y.Scale * viewportSize.Y + Frame.Position.Y.Offset
						local v77 = math.floor
						Frame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(math.floor(math.clamp(Frame.Position.X.Scale * viewportSize.X + Frame.Position.X.Offset, v73, v74)), v77(math.clamp(n7, v75, v76)))
					end

					if type(arg) ~= "table" then
						return arg
					end
					fn115(arg)
					if rawget(arg, "__senaFactoryGuarded") then
						return arg
					end

					for _, v72 in ipairs(tbl10) do
						local v73 = rawget(arg, v72)

						if type(v73) == "function" then
							rawset(arg, v72, function(...)
								v70()
								local v74 = table.pack
								local v75 = v73
								local v76

								if select("#", ...) - 0 - 1 < 0 then
									v76 = v74(v75())
								else
									v76 = v74(v75(...))
								end

								if v76.n > 0 then
									v76[1] = str(v76[1])
								end

								return table.unpack(v76, 1, v76.n)
							end)
						end
					end

					rawset(arg, "__senaFactoryGuarded", true)
					return arg
				end

				v70()

					if v78 then
						local senaV4IdentitySetter = v53.__SenaV4IdentitySetter
						local senaV4UICapabilityIdentity = v53.__SenaV4UICapabilityIdentity or 8

						local function fn115(arg)
							if senaV4IdentitySetter then
								pcall(senaV4IdentitySetter, senaV4UICapabilityIdentity)
							end

							if not v79 or not v79:Alive() then
								return
							end
							local tbl12 = {}

							for k, v80 in ipairs(arg.history) do

							end
						end)
					end
				end
